# Ads And Sponsored Experiences

_Roblox sponsored ads, ad spend/ROAS, user acquisition, off-platform promotion._

Evidence base: 1127 extracted items from 256 videos by 1Coal, Alex Builds Games, AlvinBlox, Arow, BloxxedDev, BubuTheDev, ByteBlox, CallMeDogey, Creators on the Blox, Cumin, Deconstructor of Fun, DitchyDevelopments, Duckable, Erthlord, GameMakers, GamesBeat, GnomeCode, ItsZyonic, Izzzyzzz, JustDucksey, JustVeryYellow, Last Level Studios, Lnor Roblox, LovJak, Matt The Method, MonarchDev, MonzterDEV, MoseyTube, Mulch, PineappleDev, Poke, PolarBear, Quez, Rapstrike GFX, Raul, Roblox, Roblox Creator Docs, Roblox Creators, Roblox DevForum, Roblox Investor Relations, RoyalDev, Seibo, SmartyRBX, StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data), TheDankDeveloper, TheDevBacon, TheDevKing, ThePurpleGuy, Tizzy, VuukStudios, Yann, bubDEV, janisjanis01, kra_DeZ, rasdev, tef, waveshine (2018-07-24 to 2026-10-30). Roblox changes quickly: check the date on each source and see Date context below.

## Overview
Newer indie notes add a creative-testing discipline the file lacked: most new concepts fail (85-95% in one agency's 25,000+ tests), so teams need 20-50 concepts per winner, organised as persona-by-trend matrices and judged on CTR plus installs per impression. Roblox-specific data is thin but current: 2025-26 campaigns used 3k-8k Robux budgets and an ad-credit ladder of 8 then 16 per day for two days, with one 2.34% CTR. Influencer and off-platform notes add cost traps (TikTok Spark Ads roughly double spend, no link-in-bio attribution) and advise treating influencers as a patient, permanent channel. Judge ad quality by churn per 1,000 impressions and retention, since ad networks optimise CPM, not your ARPDAU. Older 2020-22 figures (Robux-era, Facebook, IDFA) are historical context.

## Rules and findings
- **Fix retention before buying ads** (multiple-creators): Ads are the fastest way to get players but won't fix a bad game. Paid traffic is wasted on weak retention. An indie test with a weak game peaked at ~20 CCU then fell to near zero. Only advertise games whose organic retention and playtime are already good. Ads cannot reverse declining organic engagement. Sources: [DitchyDevelopments 2026-06 0:41](https://youtu.be/cfLbW2j2VLs?t=41), [DitchyDevelopments 2026-06 0:41](https://youtu.be/cfLbW2j2VLs?t=41), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/acquisition), [kra_DeZ 2026-10 8:21](https://youtu.be/lrubtajkaeI?t=501), [Tizzy 2026-05 12:12](https://youtu.be/inWzxY26NXo?t=732). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use ads to feed the algorithm, then stop** (multiple-creators): Roblox won't recommend a new game until it has retention and return-play data; a paid run buys that data. Indie creators run ads until home recommendation impressions exceed sponsored impressions (Acquisition analytics), then stop. Self-reported spend before home recs was ~$100-200. Sources: [MoseyTube 2026-04 4:47](https://youtu.be/sFr7htxsnR4?t=287), [MoseyTube 2026-06 0:19](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=19), [MoseyTube 2026-04 4:47](https://youtu.be/sFr7htxsnR4?t=287), [MoseyTube 2026-06 0:19](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=19), [Tizzy 2026-05 15:03](https://youtu.be/owMnhg3fn_M?t=903). Newest source 2026-08-25.
- **Typical modern indie budget: ~$10/day for ~10 days** (multiple-creators): MoseyTube starts at $10/day for 10 days ($100), going to $15-20/day for competitive genres. Tizzy uses $16/day for at least 3 days (ideally a week; ~11 days/$180 to clear the threshold). CallMeDogey uses $5-8/day for 7-10+ days. Alex Builds Games used $16/day for 7 days (~$120). All self-reported. Sources: [MoseyTube 2026-06 1:00](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=60), [MoseyTube 2026-06 3:34](https://youtu.be/Kh_XKYw8ocU?t=214), [MoseyTube 2026-06 1:00](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=60), [MoseyTube 2026-06 3:34](https://youtu.be/Kh_XKYw8ocU?t=214), [MoseyTube 2026-04 4:05](https://youtu.be/sFr7htxsnR4?t=245). Newest source 2026-06-29.
- **Run campaigns at least 1-2 weeks, not 1-2 days** (multiple-creators): Official Roblox guidance (2025-06): short 1-2 day campaigns don't give the system time to optimise. Official docs say allow 3-5 days for results before judging. Older indie advice of 1-day or 3-day bursts is superseded. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2025-06 14:40](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=880), [Roblox Creators 2025-06 15:22](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=922), [Roblox Creators 2025-06 14:40](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=880), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use broad targeting and let Ads Manager find players** (multiple-creators): Avoid stacking gender, age or country filters because they shrink the audience and raise cost. For a new game leave audience on All Players. Choose Maximize Plays with all regions and devices. If you do narrow, lower the daily budget and run longer. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2025-06 36:18](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=2178), [Roblox Creators 2025-06 36:18](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=2178), [Roblox Creators 2026-02 1:04](https://youtu.be/5HcH-9E7USc?t=64), [CallMeDogey 2026-03 0:00](https://youtu.be/7QfaQe4rsq8?t=0), [MoseyTube 2026-06 1:53](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=113). Newest source 2026-10-20.
- **Prefer daily budget (recent), though lifetime has defenders** (multiple-creators): Official 2025 guidance and CallMeDogey recommend a daily budget for steady flow. waveshine (2024) and kra_DeZ (2026) used lifetime budgets. Recent official advice favours daily with recommendations. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2025-06 14:40](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=880), [CallMeDogey 2026-03 6:50](https://youtu.be/7QfaQe4rsq8?t=410), [CallMeDogey 2026-03 6:50](https://youtu.be/7QfaQe4rsq8?t=410), [waveshine 2024-10 3:21](https://youtu.be/3BHo5a_OF5U?t=201), [kra_DeZ 2026-09 10:28](https://youtu.be/cJ-YzHHhQs8?t=628). Newest source 2026-10-20.
- **Optimise CTR with thumbnails and test creatives** (multiple-creators): Older DevForum advice: stop ads under 1.0% CTR, aim for ~3.0%. MoseyTube's day-one benchmark is ~2.5% CTR and ~$0.008 cost per play. Split-test thumbnails, then shift budget to the winner. kra_DeZ tests 20+ AI thumbnails. Also watch the second CTR (page click to Play) to avoid clickbait. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805), [Roblox DevForum 2023-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279), [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805), [Roblox DevForum 2023-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279), [Roblox DevForum 2023-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Split-test before big spend (launch test pattern)** (multiple-creators): DevForum 2020 pattern: split launch budget into three equal ads (clickbait, community, mobile sponsor), put daily spend on the highest-CTR for 3 days, archive the runner-up and re-run it later from revenue. kra_DeZ: ~30 credits/3 days for two thumbnails, then 90-100 credits over 4-5 days with the winner; schedule the next campaign to start when the previous ends. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805), [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805), [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805), [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805), [kra_DeZ 2026-09 11:08](https://youtu.be/cJ-YzHHhQs8?t=668). Newest source 2026-10-20.
- **Expect low-quality cold traffic and drop-off after ads end** (multiple-creators): Ads optimise for the cheapest player, so lobbies can look bot-like and early stats low. Player spikes fall back after ads end (kra_DeZ: 40-80 during ads, 5-10 after). Don't panic or restart ads repeatedly; Roblox uses a rolling 7-day window. Sources: [Tizzy 2026-05 4:45](https://youtu.be/inWzxY26NXo?t=285), [Tizzy 2026-06 8:48](https://youtu.be/Rkgbcv6omPg?t=528), [kra_DeZ 2026-09 11:08](https://youtu.be/cJ-YzHHhQs8?t=668), [kra_DeZ 2026-09 11:49](https://youtu.be/cJ-YzHHhQs8?t=709), [Tizzy 2026-05 17:23](https://youtu.be/inWzxY26NXo?t=1043). Newest source 2026-09-08.
- **Scale gradually when ROI is proven** (multiple-creators): Start small (2018 official: ~500 Robux; reinvest returns). Scale only if retention of ad users holds. InGame went from $250/day to double and triple spend while ROAS stayed above 100%. CallMeDogey reinvested earned Robux into ads. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 25:37](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=1537), [Roblox 2018-07 25:37](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=1537), [Roblox 2018-07 38:01](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=2281), [Roblox Creators 2025-10 27:20](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=1640), [Roblox 2025-09 6:49](https://youtu.be/cUxgt4asjFU?t=409). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Use Ads Manager objectives and audiences as a funnel** (multiple-creators): Maximize Plays for launches, mid-life games or updates; Drive Retention for players of the last 0-30 days; Reactivate for lapsed users; Earnings for ROAS (needs a developed economy). Example split 60/20/20. Major update on an old game: Plays + Reactivate for 1-2 weeks. Audiences: New = no play 180 days; Recent = last 30 days (10,000 minimum); Lapsed = 30-180 days (20,000 minimum). Engagement campaigns cost more per play; give them their own budget. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2025-06 28:42](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=1722), [Roblox Creators 2025-06 28:42](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=1722), [Roblox Creators 2025-06 30:04](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=1804), [Roblox Creators 2025-06 31:26](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=1886), [Roblox Creators 2025-10 16:24](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=984). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Ads don't hurt organic ranking and are excluded from RFY signals** (multiple-creators): Official: ad-acquired users' engagement, retention and spending are excluded from RFY ranking signals, so ads add incremental players. Roblox cites impression lift in 99% of 370 advertised experiences. Some RFY impressions may dip as users move to the Continue sort. Indies counter that ads don't push you up on their own. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2025-10 25:57](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=1557), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery-faq), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager), [Roblox 2025-09 24:34](https://youtu.be/cUxgt4asjFU?t=1474), [Roblox 2025-09 25:58](https://youtu.be/cUxgt4asjFU?t=1558). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Try search ads and bid on relevant keywords** (one-creator-repeated): Search ads (45M searches/day) often bring users with better engagement and monetisation than sponsored experience users. Check Audience Estimator volume, favour high-volume keywords with few bidders, bid only on relevant keywords, max 10 per Ad Set, single-keyword Ad Sets to compare. Search ads use the Visits objective, 13+, no audience tuning. Second-price auction. Sources: [Roblox 2024-09 6:43](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=403), [Roblox 2024-09 8:05](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=485), [Roblox 2024-09 6:43](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=403), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/search-ads), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/search-ads). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use ads at key moments and after updates** (multiple-creators): Ads aren't just for launch. Re-engage players at events like Halloween (D5/D7 returns). Pair a big update with big ad spend to boost early pool prioritisation. A short $10/day run can show the algorithm an updated old game. Sources: [Roblox 2024-09 23:15](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=1395), [Roblox 2024-09 25:05](https://youtu.be/QC6XWgJRJEM?t=1505), [Roblox Creators 2025-10 28:45](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=1725), [MoseyTube 2026-06 3:16](https://youtu.be/esZQX5COI_E?t=196), [MoseyTube 2026-06 3:16](https://youtu.be/esZQX5COI_E?t=196). Newest source 2026-06-10.
- **Older Robux-era sponsored ad settings (bid, spread, budgets)** (multiple-creators): 2024 waveshine/DevForum: max bid ~0.01-0.02, unspent budget refunded; ~10,000 R$ over 10-15 days, 2-4 day intervals, abandon if bad at day 5-7. 2025 AlvinBlox: 10k-30k Robux over 2-4 weeks, not 100k. SmartyRBX seeded with 1,000 Robux. waveshine warns settings change; predates the 2025 Ads Manager. Sources: [waveshine 2024-10 4:02](https://youtu.be/3BHo5a_OF5U?t=242), [waveshine 2024-10 4:02](https://youtu.be/3BHo5a_OF5U?t=242), [waveshine 2024-10 4:02](https://youtu.be/3BHo5a_OF5U?t=242), [Roblox DevForum 2024-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-make-a-popular-game/3115791), [Roblox DevForum 2024-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-make-a-popular-game/3115791). Newest source 2026-07-13.
- **Don't count on ads paying back immediately** (multiple-creators): Self-reported results: TheDankDeveloper spent ~2,800 Robux for 2,654 visits and recovered ~1,200 Robux; an earlier ~3,135 Robux run lost ~2,953. ItsZyonic's ~$300 week-1 spend didn't retain the game. Aim for break-even monetisation and high CTR. Sources: [TheDankDeveloper 2025-08 11:28](https://youtu.be/OauiTUkK7og?t=688), [TheDankDeveloper 2025-08 11:28](https://youtu.be/OauiTUkK7og?t=688), [TheDankDeveloper 2025-08 10:12](https://youtu.be/OauiTUkK7og?t=612), [TheDankDeveloper 2025-08 11:28](https://youtu.be/OauiTUkK7og?t=688), [TheDankDeveloper 2025-08 11:28](https://youtu.be/OauiTUkK7og?t=688). Newest source 2026-01-05.
- **Verify audience age and targeting quality** (multiple-creators): Roblox serves ads only to 13+ (Tizzy says ads automatically reach 16+). ItsZyonic found the sponsored audience skewed under-18 with many lying about age (2023 advice: 17+ for best return). Pick phone vs PC to match the audience. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2023-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279), [Roblox DevForum 2023-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279), [SmartyRBX 2023-01 2:01](https://youtu.be/DOMuVNlHv8Y?t=121), [ItsZyonic 2023-06 2:17](https://youtu.be/ODh2jPEGWUc?t=137), [ItsZyonic 2023-06 2:17](https://youtu.be/ODh2jPEGWUc?t=137). Newest source 2026-06-01.
- **Mix off-platform promotion and creator sponsorships with ads** (multiple-creators): Official 2018 advice and several indies: combine ads, YouTube, Twitter, TikTok. Sponsor creators matching your genre; ItsZyonic prefers sponsors over ads. SmartyRBX's tool workflow: check estimated views, set due date/notes, attach a UGC item, use credits ($5 for new accounts), or post a public listing (min $15). Counter-view: DevForum 2024 and CallMeDogey say influencer/social spikes fade and rank low. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 36:39](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=2199), [TheDevKing 2020-05 8:37](https://youtu.be/AZTDrfuk9ME?t=517), [SmartyRBX 2023-02 0:45](https://youtu.be/rdWyuDEpGyk?t=45), [SmartyRBX 2021-08 0:24](https://youtu.be/Y5lmDjab25E?t=24), [Roblox 2023-09 13:16](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=796). Newest source 2026-03-27.
- **Track channels and use deep links** (multiple-creators): Create a separate share link per off-platform channel (no limit). Group-owned games need group-account links with permission. Use deep links/launchData for attribution and promo codes. Use launch data to tailor onboarding to ad audiences, spawn players at the ad's map or grant ad-only rewards; test the launch URL first (GetJoinData in onPlayerAdded). Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/share-links), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/share-links), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/share-links), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/deeplinks), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Get campaign payment and billing rules right** (one-creator-repeated): Ads Manager needs 13+, an ad account and a payment method (card or Robux converted to ad credits). Card payments charge at $100 or month end, daily cap $300, $5 first-time charge, payment method locked after publish. Ad credits: unused credit refunded as ad credit in 24-48h (never Robux). Fraud refunds at 16 days. Cancel up to 6 hours before start. Spend earned Robux (better rate). Sources: [Roblox Creators 2026-02 0:04](https://youtu.be/5HcH-9E7USc?t=4), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Register and moderate ad assets early** (one-creator-repeated): For in-experience ads, submit campaigns at least 5 days before start; assets are moderated typically within 48 hours. Put new assets in a separate fixed-date campaign, since during review the whole campaign serves only 18+. Check AdService:GetCampaignEligibilityAsync. Self-promotional assets with calls to action also count as ads. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Follow ad policy and disclosure rules** (multiple-creators): Off-Roblox product promotion is independent advertising. Hide ads for users where PolicyService AreAdsAllowed is false (or kick if the whole game is an ad). Disclose clearly with standardised labels within 500 studs, localise disclosures, don't mimic Roblox's, no misleading content such as free Robux, no third-party ad servers. Seek legal counsel for brand deals. Under-18 creators cannot place ads (per one speaker; possibly stale). Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/advertise-on-roblox), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/advertise-on-roblox), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/advertise-on-roblox), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/comply-with-advertising-standards). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Meet in-experience ad technical specs** (one-creator-repeated): Eligibility: public game, 13+ owner, ID verification, 2FA, 2,000 unique monthly visitors, approved maturity questionnaire (resubmitting temporarily removes eligibility). Billboards 8x4.5 to 32x18 studs; no second AdGui/SurfaceGui; only scale/position/rotation may change on portal ad packages. Impression criteria: image 1s, video 0.5s, 1.5% viewport, 55 degrees, 50% pixels. Click-to-play counts at 15s. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Add rewarded video to monetise non-payers** (multiple-creators): Opt-in video (6-30s, 6-15 recommended, full-screen horizontal, 13+). Eligibility moved from 100K DAU (2025) to 2,000 unique monthly visitors with a public unrestricted game and 13+ creator (Nov 2025). Reported: 87% favourability, 1,700+ games, 27% of integrators earning >10% from it. Use Studio plugin templates, a test universe, and multi-reward rotation. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 9:38](https://youtu.be/hoMbv_R8AMU?t=578), [Roblox 2026-10 11:02](https://youtu.be/hoMbv_R8AMU?t=662), [Roblox DevForum 2025-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/more-creators-can-now-use-rewarded-video-ads/3838678), [Roblox DevForum 2025-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/more-creators-can-now-use-rewarded-video-ads/3838678), [Roblox DevForum 2025-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/more-creators-can-now-use-rewarded-video-ads/3838678). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Place rewarded video where it feels like earning** (multiple-creators): Use multiple placements in high-traffic spots (HUD, lobby, shop, loss screen), clear reward text, immediate reward, no forced ads or randomised rewards, no Robux rewards, a daily cap, boosts lasting one match/session. Time offers to moments: pre-match, revive after death, double post-match coins/XP, preserve streaks, skip cooldowns, scarcity, grind. Sources: [Roblox 2025-09 9:14](https://youtu.be/MJ-zYfL10Ew?t=554), [Roblox 2025-09 10:35](https://youtu.be/MJ-zYfL10Ew?t=635), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/obby), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/obby), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/sports). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Measure rewarded video with the API and key metrics** (multiple-creators): Track opt-in rate and daily frequency. Benchmarks vary across docs: 13%+ opt-in and 2.5+ views/user/day, or 15%+ and 3+; best-in-class 30%+ and 4+. Call GetAdAvailabilityNowAsync right before showing, not earlier, and not after an ad unless offering another. Use placement IDs and RegisterAdOpportunityAsync. If earnings fall short compute Impressions/eDAU; if zero, check availability and eligibility. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/incremental-sim), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/survival), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/incremental-sim), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/incremental-sim), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/incremental-sim). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Treat brand integrations as in-game quests, not standalone games** (multiple-creators): Brands pay for integrations inside popular games; standalone brand games usually get almost no players (over 99% per Row Monitor). Branded IP lowers UA cost but retention still needs fun. Choose brand partners that fit your vibe, weave content into the core loop; limited activations run 2-4 weeks. A lightweight trailer-style experience can be built in 1-2 weeks. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2025-09 26:32](https://youtu.be/8pruyZlxMI4?t=1592), [Deconstructor of Fun 2025-09 28:34](https://youtu.be/8pruyZlxMI4?t=1714), [Deconstructor of Fun 2025-09 29:14](https://youtu.be/8pruyZlxMI4?t=1754), [Roblox Creators 2022-05 12:12](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=732), [Roblox Creators 2022-05 12:12](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=732). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Promote with hype and launch events** (multiple-creators): Official: teaser trailers on YouTube/Twitter ending with date/time; daily short teasers across nine social accounts with a partner UGC item event; speculation content as cheap prerelease hype; portals in-experience (up to 27% of monetisation for some publishers). Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-12 14:48](https://youtu.be/bKvOPfp3oX0?t=888), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 15:29](https://youtu.be/bKvOPfp3oX0?t=929), [Roblox Creators 2024-08 19:32](https://youtu.be/2t_zhxyLOJ8?t=1172), [Roblox Creators 2023-04 0:05](https://youtu.be/vNKPlYeXj0Q?t=5), [Roblox 2023-09 14:12](https://youtu.be/yUDvI8mGnn8?t=852). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Don't over-invest in ads versus game quality** (multiple-creators): CallMeDogey ranks ads as one of the least important steps and prioritises benchmarks. AlvinBlox: use ads sparingly. SmartyRBX said 2023 ads were weaker after updates, but later used small seed budgets. A 50:50 dev/ad split was suggested by one DevForum voice. Fund ads via commissions if short. Sources: [CallMeDogey 2026-02 1:21](https://youtu.be/IQm6zE2fKUI?t=81), [AlvinBlox 2025-04 4:02](https://youtu.be/f63stwpsaXw?t=242), [SmartyRBX 2023-08 0:00](https://youtu.be/iD_8dYacd-s?t=0), [AlvinBlox 2025-04 2:00](https://youtu.be/f63stwpsaXw?t=120), [AlvinBlox 2025-04 3:18](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=198). Newest source 2026-06-01.
- **Sponsored placement and dynamics** (multiple-creators): Moving sponsored rows higher on home gave 5x impressions with constant engagement. The row is now dynamic (row 2-10) and engaging games rank higher. 38% of the top 1,000 developers used sponsored experiences in Q2 2024; 71 of the top 100 run sponsored ads. Sponsored tiles lost effectiveness in 2022 after consolidation. Sources: [Roblox 2024-09 5:22](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=322), [Roblox 2024-09 6:03](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=363), [Roblox 2024-09 5:22](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=322), [Roblox Creators 2025-06 23:53](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=1433), [Roblox Creators 2025-06 23:12](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=1392). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Judge ad traffic by day-7 retention and sessions, not impressions or clicks** (one-creator-repeated): High impressions with few plays signals a weak game. rasdev reports ads raise player counts but not ranking. Roblox tests cited by rasdev show +14% play sessions and +10% players still present a week later (median across 21 campaigns). Check these before adding spend. Sources: [rasdev 2026-10 9:36](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=576), [rasdev 2026-10 10:16](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=616), [rasdev 2026-10 10:16](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=616), [rasdev 2026-10 9:36](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=576), [rasdev 2026-10 2:17](https://youtu.be/DtdGtvALhCA?t=137). Newest source 2026-10-17.
- **Kill weak ads early using CTR and play-rate thresholds** (one-creator-repeated): Check CTR and playthrough rate after about one day. janisjanis treats a 0.3% CTR with 0.07 play rate as a failing ad, aims for over 1% playthrough, and calls 2.25% CTR only average. Ads in the 2.5-3% CTR range were judged good. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-12 4:45](https://youtu.be/9EXrUN7PUrE?t=285), [janisjanis01 2025-07 3:59](https://youtu.be/Sgs36l6K5EU?t=239), [janisjanis01 2025-09 11:31](https://youtu.be/YVKHlrlWn-k?t=691), [janisjanis01 2024-12 4:45](https://youtu.be/9EXrUN7PUrE?t=285), [janisjanis01 2025-09 12:12](https://youtu.be/YVKHlrlWn-k?t=732). Newest source 2026-02-08.
- **Pay only while engaged players keep arriving, then stop at the target** (multiple-creators): Yann ran ads 19 days at $20/day and said he should have stopped sooner. rasdev warns the new engagement goal is in beta and can overspend, so pause it manually once the engaged-player target is hit. Sources: [Yann 2026-07 2:11](https://youtu.be/Bw2ty2T46z4?t=131), [Yann 2026-07 2:11](https://youtu.be/Bw2ty2T46z4?t=131), [rasdev 2026-10 5:26](https://youtu.be/RC19PKdRt1Y?t=326), [Yann 2026-07 2:11](https://youtu.be/Bw2ty2T46z4?t=131). Newest source 2026-10-23.
- **Do not advertise a buggy or unstable build** (multiple-creators): Duckable spent 32,000 Robux on a game that broke, leaving the balance negative. Ads cannot fix a game problem and players will still leave. Stabilise and retain first. Sources: [Duckable 2026-05 23:35](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=1415), [rasdev 2026-07 2:43](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=163), [rasdev 2026-07 2:43](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=163). Newest source 2026-07-08.
- **Set the ad goal to Maximize Plays and keep device and region targeting at defaults** (multiple-creators): bubDEV and Yann both use Maximize Plays with all devices and all regions. bubDEV found narrowing to computer, mobile or tablet cut reach significantly. Yann says more audiences lifted CTR from 3.3% to about 4.5%. Sources: [bubDEV 2025-12 3:29](https://youtu.be/7z8rfg_jBuk?t=209), [bubDEV 2025-12 3:29](https://youtu.be/7z8rfg_jBuk?t=209), [Yann 2026-05 2:02](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=122), [Yann 2026-07 0:30](https://youtu.be/Bw2ty2T46z4?t=30). Newest source 2026-07-23.
- **Size the budget by game type and trend urgency** (one-creator-repeated): Yann's three factors: trend urgency, whether the game needs multiplayer, and genre supply versus demand. Simulators need less spend than multiplayer games like tag or knockout. Niche genres like racing need more. Sources: [Yann 2026-05 2:43](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=163), [Yann 2026-05 3:25](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=205). Newest source 2026-05-30.
- **Track ad return in Robux, not just spend** (multiple-creators): Duckable spent 37,000 Robux on ads and got about 10,000 back in the first 24-hour game. Yann spent 100,000 Robux (380 credits) over 30 days and got 30,000 back. Calculate return on every campaign before scaling. Sources: [Duckable 2026-05 22:12](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=1332), [Duckable 2026-05 22:12](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=1332), [Yann 2026-07 0:30](https://youtu.be/Bw2ty2T46z4?t=30), [Yann 2026-07 0:30](https://youtu.be/Bw2ty2T46z4?t=30), [janisjanis01 2026-08 17:44](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=1064). Newest source 2026-08-30.
- **Check spend against budget every day** (single-mention): janisjanis found some ads stopped before spending their ad credits. Confirm each campaign is delivering its budget daily rather than assuming it is running at full pace. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-11 15:04](https://youtu.be/QnAoCsC-4ig?t=904). Newest source 2025-11-02.
- **Seed the algorithm with small consistent spend plus off-platform players** (one-creator-repeated): rasdev suggests 10k-30k Robux over 2-4 weeks of small, steady ad spend to generate data, not one big spend. Pair it with friends, dev communities, Discord, TikTok or YouTube. The goal is data the algorithm can act on. Sources: [rasdev 2026-04 2:01](https://youtu.be/G15TVsyC61g?t=121), [rasdev 2026-04 2:01](https://youtu.be/G15TVsyC61g?t=121), [rasdev 2026-05 7:45](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=465), [rasdev 2026-04 2:01](https://youtu.be/G15TVsyC61g?t=121), [rasdev 2026-04 2:01](https://youtu.be/G15TVsyC61g?t=121). Newest source 2026-05-03.
- **Verify campaign eligibility before creating a campaign** (single-mention): Call campaign-options for the universe first. A NO_PERMISSION or BLOCKED response means the universe cannot advertise. Confirm this before writing any campaign-creation code or spending. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Send an idempotency key on campaign creation** (single-mention): Include a UUID in x-idempotency-key. Replaying the same key with an identical body within 24 hours returns the original campaign, which prevents duplicate campaigns and double spend. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Poll campaign status after creation; new campaigns start IN_REVIEW** (single-mention): Campaigns stay IN_REVIEW until ad-policy review completes. Poll campaigns:batchGetStatus (up to 100 IDs per call) until SERVING, NOT_SERVING or REJECTED. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Set campaign length with durationInDays, not endTime** (single-mention): endTime returns a 400 in v1. durationInDays has a maximum of 3650 days. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Raise budgets carefully and expect decreases to wait for midnight** (single-mention): Budget increases apply immediately. Decreases apply at the next midnight in the billing account time zone. Raise budgets in small steps to avoid surprise spend. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use PAUSED to stop spend temporarily; CANCELLED is permanent** (single-mention): CANCELLED cannot be undone. Use PAUSED to halt spend and ACTIVE to resume a paused campaign. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use empty targeting arrays deliberately** (single-mention): In targeting arrays (ageGroups, countries, devices), an empty array means all for that dimension. ageGroups options are AGE_13_17, AGE_18_24 and AGE_25_PLUS. Narrow only on purpose. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Upload creatives as Open Cloud image assets** (single-mention): Creatives must be Open Cloud image assets uploaded via the Assets API, then referenced by ID in creativeAssetIds. Each campaign allows up to 10 thumbnails. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising), [rasdev 2026-10 10:57](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=657). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Target the Kids and Select engaged-player threshold at 250** (multiple-creators): rasdev says the threshold for Roblox Kids and Select campaigns fell from 500 to 250 engaged players in August 2026. tef cites 250 as the active-player threshold for targeting under-16 audiences. Verify against the current docs before planning around it. Sources: [rasdev 2026-10 10:57](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=657), [rasdev 2026-10 10:57](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=657), [tef 2026-10 14:28](https://youtu.be/OfObWnrucRY?t=868). Newest source 2026-10-23.
- **Expect a slow ramp while the algorithm learns the audience** (multiple-creators): tef says the ad algorithm needs time to learn who wants to play the game before it pushes to the right players. janisjanis reported a surge after a scaled campaign, so ramp timing varies. Allow the learning period before judging a campaign. Sources: [tef 2026-10 14:28](https://youtu.be/OfObWnrucRY?t=868), [tef 2026-10 14:28](https://youtu.be/OfObWnrucRY?t=868), [janisjanis01 2025-09 12:26](https://youtu.be/YVKHlrlWn-k?t=746). Newest source 2026-10-23.
- **Pick run length and budget for a new game, then reassess** (multiple-creators): bubDEV sets a 250 lifetime budget (range 150-400) for 5-7 days on new games and says longer runs are unnecessary. Yann says reassess by 2 weeks. Duckable spent $16/day for 10 days and says a big budget is not required for a game to take off. Sources: [bubDEV 2025-12 3:29](https://youtu.be/7z8rfg_jBuk?t=209), [bubDEV 2025-12 2:09](https://youtu.be/7z8rfg_jBuk?t=129), [Yann 2026-05 7:30](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=450), [Duckable 2026-05 9:38](https://youtu.be/X9YYjb_mY5w?t=578), [Duckable 2026-05 9:38](https://youtu.be/X9YYjb_mY5w?t=578). Newest source 2026-05-30.
- **Budget at a small scale first, scale only after proof** (one-creator-repeated): janisjanis moved from a 4,500 Robux first ad to a 10,000 Robux campaign only after early results, and that campaign brought the game 72 players and algorithm entry. Repeat small tests before major spend. Sources: [janisjanis01 2023-10 0:25](https://youtu.be/5E5pj7CB4mM?t=25), [janisjanis01 2025-09 12:26](https://youtu.be/YVKHlrlWn-k?t=746), [janisjanis01 2025-09 11:31](https://youtu.be/YVKHlrlWn-k?t=691), [janisjanis01 2025-09 12:26](https://youtu.be/YVKHlrlWn-k?t=746), [janisjanis01 2026-06 10:06](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=606). Newest source 2026-06-06.
- **Set win criteria before the campaign goes live** (one-creator-repeated): Write targets before spending: beat the Robux spent, get players returning (not just plays), and pay back ad spend. Check them at day 7. Visit-heavy campaigns with no returners (MonarchDev, Quez) missed all three. Sources: [MonarchDev 2026-10 10:36](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=636), [MonarchDev 2026-09 2:43](https://youtu.be/LD7LGP10dRc?t=163), [MonarchDev 2026-10 10:36](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=636), [Quez 2025-03 3:18](https://youtu.be/QPxXr57M-xM?t=198), [Quez 2025-03 4:00](https://youtu.be/QPxXr57M-xM?t=240). Newest source 2026-10-03.
- **Use Sponsored Items for Marketplace items only; use Ads Manager or the API for experiences** (multiple-creators): Official docs: Sponsored Items promote Marketplace items only. Advertise an experience through Ads Manager or the Advertising API. Erthlord's UGC push (200 Robux per item across 17 items, 3,400 Robux) showed no next-day sales lift, with 24 sales by day 10, so measure over several days. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items), [Erthlord 2026-07 9:32](https://youtu.be/57GCHQtyuZQ?t=572), [Erthlord 2026-07 12:21](https://youtu.be/57GCHQtyuZQ?t=741). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Set audience, name and daily budget when creating a sponsored item** (single-mention): Creating a sponsored item requires a target audience (gender, age, platform) and a descriptive name, which only you can see. Minimum bid is 10 Robux per day, and ad cycles run 24 hours to 28 days. Display share scales with your daily budget relative to rival bidders. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Cancel early to recover unused full days** (multiple-creators): Roblox holds the full cycle amount when you press Run. Cancelling refunds each full day remaining, not partial days (a 3-day cycle cancelled on day 1 refunds 2 days). Cancel as soon as results are clear, using PAUSED or cancel steps as appropriate. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/we%E2%80%99ve-rebuilt-sponsored-games/832597), [Roblox DevForum 2020-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/we%E2%80%99ve-rebuilt-sponsored-games/832597), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Check age rating before planning ad-driven acquisition** (multiple-creators): A 2023 DevForum post says 17+ experiences could not use Immersive Ads, User Ads or Sponsored Experiences at launch, and the ad audience floor is over 13. Official 2026 guidance: promoting to users over 16 and getting engagement from age-checked 16+ users speeds Kids/Select evaluation. Re-verify the 17+ rule, which is dated. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2023-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/create-experiences-for-people-17-and-older-on-roblox/2432184), [Roblox DevForum 2023-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/create-experiences-for-people-17-and-older-on-roblox/2432184), [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/alternate-publishing-requirements-for-roblox-kids-and-select/4630944). Newest source 2026-05-11.
- **Match audience to goal: All Players for new players, Recent Players for lapsed ones** (multiple-creators): Arow sets the goal to Plays and the audience to All Players, and uses Recent Players to bring back lapsed players. Gender and basic age targeting can narrow spend to likely players (DevForum 2020). Treat this as a test, since broad targeting is the default in other guidance. Sources: [Arow 2026-05 0:40](https://youtu.be/BxK28jVj1MY?t=40), [Roblox DevForum 2020-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/we%E2%80%99ve-rebuilt-sponsored-games/832597), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Fund ads with Robux or Wallet, and budget in ad credits** (multiple-creators): Wallet funds can be spent directly on ads, in addition to Robux. Ad credits can be bought with Robux: about 1,000 Robux for roughly $3.80 of credits. Minimum credit purchase was 10 credits (2,850 Robux) and minimum daily budget 5 in a 2025 campaign. Convert to Robux before judging cost. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505), [MonarchDev 2026-10 10:36](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=636), [MonarchDev 2026-10 10:36](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=636), [JustDucksey 2025-02 0:00](https://youtu.be/nQPm7gmRBzs?t=0), [JustDucksey 2025-02 0:44](https://youtu.be/nQPm7gmRBzs?t=44). Newest source 2026-10-03.
- **Finalise thumbnails and icons before launch** (single-mention): Raul reports Roblox does not let you swap a thumbnail on an active ad; you must cancel and restart, losing momentum. Test icons and thumbnails before spending. VuukStudios advises swapping thumbnails mid-run, so verify in Ads Manager which is true for your campaign type. Sources: [Raul 2026-08 4:04](https://youtu.be/7vYv8DA7pqU?t=244), [VuukStudios 2025-11 12:55](https://youtu.be/AbjI7ygMnao?t=775). Newest source 2026-08-06.
- **Check pacing in the first hours, not just the daily total** (multiple-creators): Short bursts deliver most impressions at the start and die within minutes. Lifetime budgets can burn out in hours instead of spreading over the window (GnomeCode). Check spend and CCU within the first hour or two. GnomeCode's CPM settled at 0.08 credits per 1,000 impressions after that point. Sources: [BloxxedDev 2024-08 0:00](https://youtu.be/fDJoaxTuRf4?t=0), [GnomeCode 2024-11 3:05](https://youtu.be/g9UbIrR0RbU?t=185), [GnomeCode 2024-11 3:20](https://youtu.be/g9UbIrR0RbU?t=200). Newest source 2024-11-07.
- **Stop paid spend at the home-recommendation crossover** (multiple-creators): Seibo cut sponsored credits as organic home-recommendation players grew (14/day, then 8-10, then 3/day, near zero by Oct 1). VuukStudios turned ads off once home recommendations took over. Don't advertise saturated games: GnomeCode paid about 2 cents per play for a well-known, low-monetised game. Sources: [VuukStudios 2026-07 12:26](https://youtu.be/Oo3sCq-TQXs?t=746), [Seibo 2026-10 0:43](https://youtu.be/FmAD02FS_3k?t=43), [Seibo 2026-10 0:43](https://youtu.be/FmAD02FS_3k?t=43), [Seibo 2026-10 0:43](https://youtu.be/FmAD02FS_3k?t=43), [Seibo 2026-10 1:26](https://youtu.be/FmAD02FS_3k?t=86). Newest source 2026-10-04.
- **Build in-experience video ads without spammy placement** (one-creator-repeated): Put an Ad GUI in a part, scale it, set the face and adornee, then enable video ads. Set a fallback image so the slot shows something when no ad loads. Weave ads into in-world screens rather than spawn billboards, avoid clickable slots that send players to unrelated experiences, and add them only once player numbers are meaningful. Sources: [ByteBlox 2024-02 0:24](https://youtu.be/okGvZjneq60?t=24), [ByteBlox 2024-02 2:12](https://youtu.be/okGvZjneq60?t=132), [ByteBlox 2024-02 2:54](https://youtu.be/okGvZjneq60?t=174), [ByteBlox 2024-02 3:36](https://youtu.be/okGvZjneq60?t=216), [ByteBlox 2024-02 4:18](https://youtu.be/okGvZjneq60?t=258). Newest source 2024-02-20.
- **Pre-roll video ads need no code** (single-mention): Official: pre-roll video ads (coming soon) need no code. Roblox optimises delivery and shares revenue. Roblox Plus subscribers won't see them. Plan for pre-roll as a passive revenue source, not a build task, and check rollout status before depending on it. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880). Newest source 2026-09-11.
- **For group-owned games, track ad revenue under the group** (single-mention): When the experience is group-owned, check revenue under the group rather than your personal account. Otherwise ad return will look like zero. Sources: [Raul 2026-08 2:44](https://youtu.be/7vYv8DA7pqU?t=164). Newest source 2026-08-06.
- **Reinvest early earnings in a small ad test** (single-mention): JustDucksey suggests reinvesting early Robux earnings into ads for a small game to test whether it can attract players. Keep it small and check retention, since this creator's paid players converted poorly. Sources: [JustDucksey 2025-04 3:31](https://youtu.be/qxE7JhikvwY?t=211), [JustDucksey 2025-02 5:36](https://youtu.be/nQPm7gmRBzs?t=336). Newest source 2025-04-19.
- **Run a limited-time test with about $100-250 of ad space** (single-mention): Roblox Creator Docs (Oct 2026) suggests spending roughly $100-250 USD on Roblox ad space to drive traffic to a limited-time test window. Use it to validate a launch window before committing a larger budget. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Confirm monetisation is live before buying traffic** (multiple-creators): Mulch spent 100,000 Robux over ~2 months and got back 731 Robux in revenue. PolarBear ran ads on a joke game with no game passes. ThePurpleGuy notes spend is committed once credits are used. Check passes, dev products and retention first. Sources: [Mulch 2025-06 9:35](https://youtu.be/B2vC4-QArWg?t=575), [Mulch 2025-06 0:00](https://youtu.be/B2vC4-QArWg?t=0), [PolarBear 2024-02 0:00](https://youtu.be/tNkQSpq2SXg?t=0), [ThePurpleGuy 2026-05 0:00](https://youtu.be/JXg2ACdk_wM?t=0). Newest source 2026-05-13.
- **Turn off auto-reload and cap spend with a daily budget** (one-creator-repeated): Auto-reload can cause unplanned top-ups. Use a custom daily budget (7 to 12 credits/day were cited) and a fixed run length (LovJak used 10 credits/day for 3 days). Confirm both in Ads Manager before launch. Sources: [LovJak 2026-07 4:06](https://youtu.be/feVPsViqceo?t=246), [LovJak 2026-07 4:06](https://youtu.be/feVPsViqceo?t=246), [LovJak 2026-07 2:41](https://youtu.be/Y602HH_KdOc?t=161), [LovJak 2026-07 2:41](https://youtu.be/Y602HH_KdOc?t=161), [LovJak 2026-07 4:06](https://youtu.be/feVPsViqceo?t=246). Newest source 2026-07-31.
- **Plan for unused credits, which cannot be refunded to Robux** (multiple-creators): PolarBear left about half of the credits bought unused, and unused credits cannot be refunded to Robux. Buy in smaller batches and reconcile spend against credits. Cancelling early still recovers unused full days. Sources: [PolarBear 2024-03 3:17](https://youtu.be/bwMUqu-wWpw?t=197), [PolarBear 2024-03 3:44](https://youtu.be/bwMUqu-wWpw?t=224), [LovJak 2026-05 6:50](https://youtu.be/FpbZRgRHYjQ?t=410), [LovJak 2026-05 8:55](https://youtu.be/FpbZRgRHYjQ?t=535). Newest source 2026-05-01.
- **Bid per play above the 0.01 floor, usually 0.02 to 0.03** (multiple-creators): Ad bids are cost-per-play. Below 0.01 the ad will not show, and higher bids win better auction placement. PolarBear used ~0.02 for small budgets and 0.05 to 0.06 above roughly 15,000 Robux. BubuTheDev says 0.01 to 0.03 is enough. Sources: [PolarBear 2024-03 3:36](https://youtu.be/sk8HQDgJyE0?t=216), [PolarBear 2024-03 4:16](https://youtu.be/sk8HQDgJyE0?t=256), [PolarBear 2024-03 3:36](https://youtu.be/sk8HQDgJyE0?t=216), [BubuTheDev 2025-03 2:04](https://youtu.be/0NK_JBYEILA?t=124), [BubuTheDev 2025-03 1:22](https://youtu.be/0NK_JBYEILA?t=82). Newest source 2025-03-16.
- **Schedule spend for peak days (weekends, Friday to Sunday)** (multiple-creators): PolarBear schedules sponsored campaigns for weekends when more players are online. LovJak targeted Friday to Sunday and started on a Friday at 4pm. Align run windows with your own concurrency data. Sources: [PolarBear 2024-03 6:29](https://youtu.be/sk8HQDgJyE0?t=389), [LovJak 2026-05 6:50](https://youtu.be/FpbZRgRHYjQ?t=410), [LovJak 2026-05 6:50](https://youtu.be/FpbZRgRHYjQ?t=410). Newest source 2026-05-01.
- **Start geographically broad but narrow to your spenders** (multiple-creators): BubuTheDev starts with US and Canada, then narrows gender and age to the group that actually spends Robux (e.g. 13-17 or 18-24). Lnor's verified 18-24 PC and mobile targeting lifted CTR from ~1.5% to over 8% in a day. Test to confirm for your game. Sources: [BubuTheDev 2025-03 1:22](https://youtu.be/0NK_JBYEILA?t=82), [BubuTheDev 2025-03 1:22](https://youtu.be/0NK_JBYEILA?t=82), [Lnor Roblox 2026-07 6:17](https://youtu.be/U6gv03QGcDs?t=377), [Lnor Roblox 2026-07 6:17](https://youtu.be/U6gv03QGcDs?t=377). Newest source 2026-07-26.
- **Verify that ad clicks can become plays** (one-creator-repeated): Lnor found unverified users were clicking ads they could not enter, so the spend was wasted on clicks that could not become plays. Target verified players and confirm the join path works before launch. Sources: [Lnor Roblox 2026-07 4:55](https://youtu.be/U6gv03QGcDs?t=295), [Lnor Roblox 2026-07 6:17](https://youtu.be/U6gv03QGcDs?t=377), [Lnor Roblox 2026-07 6:17](https://youtu.be/U6gv03QGcDs?t=377). Newest source 2026-07-26.
- **Make the ad promise match what the game delivers** (single-mention): Lnor saw higher playtime but worse day-1 retention from ad traffic, which pointed to an audience or messaging mismatch. Make thumbnails and copy describe what the first minutes actually deliver, then judge by D1 retention. Sources: [Lnor Roblox 2026-05 2:02](https://youtu.be/QOS7dQowyRk?t=122). Newest source 2026-05-10.
- **Expect narrow reach until about 500 highly engaged players** (multiple-creators): LovJak needed about 500 highly engaged players to unlock all-ages ad targeting and had 300. RoyalDev also cites a 500-player engagement barrier. Until you pass it, expect ads to reach a narrow audience. Sources: [LovJak 2026-08 7:31](https://youtu.be/KZ_mxVrvHCI?t=451), [LovJak 2026-08 7:31](https://youtu.be/KZ_mxVrvHCI?t=451), [RoyalDev 2026-08 0:40](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=40), [LovJak 2026-09 5:12](https://youtu.be/VHdyHvHOId8?t=312). Newest source 2026-09-12.
- **Run campaigns continuously; do not pull and repost** (multiple-creators): Roblox spends about 24 hours learning after publish, and pulling and reposting resets that learning. RoyalDev's untouched ad drove ~8,000 visits versus ~1,000 to 4,000 when cycled on and off. Let it run before judging. Sources: [RoyalDev 2026-08 2:43](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=163), [RoyalDev 2026-08 14:17](https://youtu.be/RXmiJkaZWV4?t=857), [RoyalDev 2026-08 2:43](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=163), [RoyalDev 2026-08 0:40](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=40), [RoyalDev 2026-08 4:06](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=246). Newest source 2026-08-15.
- **Reuse the thumbnail that already won** (multiple-creators): LovJak reuses the thumbnail that previously performed best (a banana thumbnail) in new ad campaigns. Lnor saw a slight CTR gain from AI-redone thumbnails. Establish a proven baseline before testing variants. Sources: [LovJak 2026-09 2:46](https://youtu.be/VHdyHvHOId8?t=166), [Lnor Roblox 2026-05 1:22](https://youtu.be/QOS7dQowyRk?t=82). Newest source 2026-09-12.
- **Build a quick cutout ad image with a play button** (one-creator-repeated): PolarBear makes an ad image in Canva using a recognisable figure with the background removed (remove.bg) plus a play button, in about a minute. Pick a face whose fans overlap your game's genre. Sources: [PolarBear 2024-02 2:45](https://youtu.be/SNauF9OES7E?t=165), [PolarBear 2024-02 2:45](https://youtu.be/SNauF9OES7E?t=165). Newest source 2024-02-08.
- **Split large budgets across several days** (multiple-creators): Mulch spread spend across days to test performance over time. Lnor warned against spending the whole budget at once after burning ~8,000 Robux untested. Spread spend and check results between tranches. Sources: [Mulch 2025-06 0:41](https://youtu.be/B2vC4-QArWg?t=41), [Lnor Roblox 2026-05 2:02](https://youtu.be/QOS7dQowyRk?t=122). Newest source 2026-05-10.
- **Exclude console players if you do not want them** (single-mention): PolarBear removed console from the ad set to narrow the audience. Check the platform setting in the ad set rather than assuming all platforms are included. Sources: [PolarBear 2024-03 2:26](https://youtu.be/sk8HQDgJyE0?t=146). Newest source 2024-03-28.
- **Choose the destination experience before submitting** (multiple-creators): PolarBear says ads cannot be modified after submission, so the destination experience must be correct at creation. JustVeryYellow's walkthrough also picks the experience first, then objective, budget and targeting. Sources: [PolarBear 2024-03 5:06](https://youtu.be/sk8HQDgJyE0?t=306), [JustVeryYellow 2025-11 0:40](https://youtu.be/rzB8CcRzFK8?t=40). Newest source 2025-11-16.
- **Check age rules for European audiences** (multiple-creators): Ads targeting European regions require accounts aged 13+ due to local laws. Creating ads requires 13+ and a verified account. Plan audience and region targeting accordingly. Sources: [JustVeryYellow 2025-11 1:21](https://youtu.be/rzB8CcRzFK8?t=81), [BubuTheDev 2025-03 0:00](https://youtu.be/0NK_JBYEILA?t=0). Newest source 2025-11-16.
- **Treat Roblox sponsored-event placement as a separate, invitation-based channel** (single-mention): Last Level Studios was approached by Roblox for sponsored events (Fruity Pebbles, Imagination). Being placed in platform events drove large prize-claim numbers (1.9M wings for Fruity Pebbles versus 70k prizes). This was a 2021 invitation, not a self-serve option. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2021-08 12:24](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=744), [Last Level Studios 2021-08 12:24](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=744). Newest source 2021-08-27.
- **Test 18+ (O18) audiences in paid acquisition where policy allows** (single-mention): Roblox Investor Relations (Sep 2026) reports paid UA targeting O18 users retains, engages and monetizes better than organic signups. Test an O18 segment against an all-player baseline on day-7 retention and Robux per user. Keep the ROI bar high while scaling. Confirm age rating and audience rules first. Sources: [Roblox Investor Relations 2026-09](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm). Newest source 2026-09-30.
- **Don't credit a traffic spike to ads before checking for events** (single-mention): A StudioKrew agency case (Speed Keyboard Escape, Aug 2026) attributes its record spikes to admin abuse events, not ads. Check the event calendar, admin actions and promotions before reading a CCU or visit spike as ad performance. Self-reported, low-advice source. Sources: [StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) 2026-08](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-august-2026/). Newest source 2026-08-20.
- **Ask Roblox about co-marketing and UA support before funding off-platform acquisition alone** (single-mention): Roblox reportedly offers on- and off-platform user acquisition support and co-marketing programs that can amplify a creator's off-platform acquisition spend. This is indie-reported (GamesBeat, 2026-03), so confirm eligibility and terms directly with Roblox before budgeting around it. Sources: [GamesBeat 2026-03](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-unveils-jumpstart-and-incubator-programs-to-help-creators-expand-possibilities-exclusive/). Newest source 2026-03-09.
- **Do not count Roblox Plus subscribers in pre-roll reach** (single-mention): Per the official DevForum post (2026-09), pre-roll video ads are still 'coming soon'. Roblox optimizes their delivery and shares revenue with creators, and Roblox Plus subscribers won't see them. Model pre-roll impressions and revenue with that exclusion. The no-code setup is already covered by an existing rule. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880). Newest source 2026-09-11.
- **Use paid ads to win back lapsed players, not only to acquire new ones** (one-creator-repeated): Deconstructor of Fun reports a 40,000-player lapsed-player campaign (two shooter co-op campaigns) where 50% returned within one week at under $2 cost per activation. Claimed, not independently verified. Extends the existing 'Recent Players for lapsed ones' audience rule with a measured reactivation benchmark. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 37:13](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2233), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 36:32](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2192). Newest source 2026-10-26.
- **Judge ad impact on repeated exposure, not last touch** (single-mention): Deconstructor of Fun cites roughly eight impressions before a player takes an action. Single-click attribution will undercount ads that build awareness across multiple touches. Compare cohorts over a window rather than crediting the final click. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 41:14](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2474). Newest source 2026-10-26.
- **Use Discord quest ad buys for existing games as a reactivation channel** (one-creator-repeated): Discord campaigns on existing games split roughly 50% new users and 50% lapsed reengagement, and about half the spend reactivates lapsed players. Treat this as a win-back channel alongside acquisition, not a pure new-user channel. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 6:46](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=406), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 39:51](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2391), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 6:46](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=406). Newest source 2026-10-29.
- **Scale user acquisition only when ROAS is positive** (single-mention): Deconstructor of Fun warns that creator activations driving download uplift do not prove that paid ads are profitable. Only increase UA spend after measured return exceeds cost. Strengthens the existing 'Scale gradually when ROI is proven' rule. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-10 19:02](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=1142). Newest source 2026-10-22.
- **Vary audience and avatar per product, as media buyers do** (single-mention): Alex Builds Games advises treating each game as a media-buying test, varying audience and avatar per product to find winners. This suits multi-game publishers and differs from the existing 'broad targeting' default. Test a small set of audiences before committing budget. Sources: [Alex Builds Games 2026-10 13:01](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=781). Newest source 2026-10-09.
- **Expect most creatives to fail; produce 20-50 concepts per winner** (multiple-creators): Across 25,000+ multivariate tests, 85-95% of new concepts failed to beat the best ad, and winners hit roughly 1 in 20 to 1 in 30. Size creative volume to monthly spend: too many wastes creatives, too few causes fatigue and running out. Sources: [GameMakers 2022-03 1:26](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=86), [GameMakers 2022-03 1:26](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=86), [GameMakers 2022-03 1:26](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=86), [GameMakers 2022-03 6:39](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=399), [GameMakers 2022-03 0:03](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=3). Newest source 2026-02-20.
- **Build a persona-by-trend concept matrix before producing ads** (one-creator-repeated): Segment players into personas by motivation (e.g. excitement, success, relaxation). Cross these rows with competitor creative trends as columns. Each cell is one concept with a defined intent, so ads speak to a specific motivation rather than a vague one. Sources: [GameMakers 2022-03 5:58](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=358), [GameMakers 2022-03 11:52](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=712). Newest source 2022-03-01.
- **Test fast and judge creatives on CTR and installs per impression (IPM)** (single-mention): Do not over-polish. A rough, wacky ad can beat a careful one. Rank each creative on click-through rate and IPM (impressions per install) before scaling it. Sources: [GameMakers 2022-03 26:58](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=1618), [janisjanis01 2025-11 14:16](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=856). Newest source 2025-11-23.
- **Keep a winning ad in its own campaign; never mix challengers into it** (one-creator-repeated): On Facebook, a champion's spend history cannot be removed, so a challenger added to the same campaign almost always loses. Run challengers in separate campaigns and compare them on equal footing. Sources: [GameMakers 2022-03 33:52](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=2032), [GameMakers 2022-03 33:52](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=2032). Newest source 2022-03-01.
- **Check placement watch time and completion, not just installs** (single-mention): A placement's skip rules determine how long viewers actually see the ad. Compare watch time and completion per placement alongside install rate before deciding where budget goes. Sources: [GameMakers 2021-11 12:45](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=765). Newest source 2021-11-10.
- **Mine past campaign data for patterns, then validate in a controlled test** (one-creator-repeated): Use legacy campaign data to find placement and duration sweet spots, and use them to rule out weak placements and prioritise creative production. Confirm any pattern in a controlled test before relying on it for budget decisions. Sources: [GameMakers 2021-11 26:44](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=1604), [GameMakers 2021-11 39:05](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=2345), [GameMakers 2021-11 14:32](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=872). Newest source 2021-11-10.
- **Measure CPI against forecast LTV early, and re-check as CPI rises** (one-creator-repeated): Spend a small UA budget early to measure the CPI vs LTV gap, and track retention at the same time. CPI rises over time from audience saturation, new competing apps and rivals improving, so standing still erodes profitability. Sources: [GameMakers 2025-02 12:12](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=732), [GameMakers 2022-08 38:20](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=2300). Newest source 2025-02-04.
- **Use churn per 1,000 impressions as an ad-quality check** (one-creator-repeated): Losing about 1 user per 1,000 impressions is fine; 100 per 1,000 is very bad. Ad networks optimise for CPM, not your ARPDAU, and the publisher pays the retention cost, so do not raise monetisation by letting retention fall. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-02 8:29](https://youtu.be/Z_UOQ2nSZq4?t=509), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-02 7:29](https://youtu.be/Z_UOQ2nSZq4?t=449), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-02 9:51](https://youtu.be/Z_UOQ2nSZq4?t=591). Newest source 2026-02-20.
- **Test a new country with a small paid campaign before any influencer push** (single-mention): Run a small paid UA campaign in the new country first. Scale to influencers only if retention, CPI or ROI look good, then move the market to always-on. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 30:49](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=1849). Newest source 2026-06-01.
- **Do not use TikTok for influencer attribution without budgeting Spark Ads** (one-creator-repeated): TikTok influencer posts have no link-in-bio or sticker link, so attribution needs paid Spark Ads on top of the creator fee. That doubles the spend, so avoid TikTok when you need trackable installs. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 34:16](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2056), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 34:16](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2056). Newest source 2026-06-01.
- **Vet local agencies through case studies** (single-mention): Agencies know local creators and often get better rates, and they reduce coordination overhead for small teams. Judge them on case studies before signing. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 31:30](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=1890). Newest source 2026-06-01.
- **Treat influencer marketing as a permanent pillar with set expectations** (one-creator-repeated): First campaigns are for learning, and influencer marketing needs budget and time before it becomes profitable. Cutting spend increased install decline in one 10-year-old game. Giveaway campaigns show a lower organic share than non-giveaway ones. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 43:13](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2593), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 4:47](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=287), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-06 10:59](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=659). Newest source 2026-06-01.
- **Make the game public before the first ad campaign** (single-mention): Publish the game, then run the first campaign with goal Players, an all-player audience, auto-reload off, and 2-3 thumbnails at 8 ad credits per day for 2 days. Sources: [janisjanis01 2026-08 5:24](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=324). Newest source 2026-08-02.
- **Step budget up to 16 ad credits per day only after the first campaign shows traction** (one-creator-repeated): After a first campaign at 8 ad credits per day for 2 days, launch a second at 16 ad credits per day for 2 days if traction shows. This is a controlled ladder, not a jump to full budget. Sources: [janisjanis01 2026-08 6:05](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=365), [janisjanis01 2026-08 5:24](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=324). Newest source 2026-08-02.
- **Stress-test ROAS targets against CPM swings** (single-mention): A hyper-casual example targeting about 110% ROAS over 30 days was broken by a 30% CPM drop. Model your return target with a downside CPM scenario before committing budget. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-02 14:59](https://youtu.be/Z_UOQ2nSZq4?t=899). Newest source 2026-02-20.
- **Expect paid installs to lag impressions and plan for more noise** (single-mention): Paid installs rose about 10% while impressions rose about 20%, implying more noise per conversion. Use larger samples and confirm trends over several days before acting. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-02 22:21](https://youtu.be/Z_UOQ2nSZq4?t=1341). Newest source 2026-02-20.
- **Confirm no unintended duplicate campaigns run concurrently** (single-mention): One indie accidentally ran two launch ads at once, spending 5,000 Robux across both before the mistake was noticed. Before launch and after edits, list every active campaign and check for overlaps so spend and results stay attributable. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-06 14:02](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=842). Newest source 2025-06-15.
- **Prioritise a few first-party ad platforms over new networks and middlemen (mobile UA)** (one-creator-repeated): For mobile-app user acquisition, not Roblox-specific: master a few first-party channels (Google, Meta, TikTok, Snapchat, Apple Search) plus rewarded networks. Cut or limit DSP middlemen, whose reported 20-40% margin is hard to justify against using first-party algorithms directly. Sources: [GameMakers 2024-10 25:11](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1511), [GameMakers 2024-10 26:35](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1595), [GameMakers 2024-10 53:20](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=3200). Newest source 2024-10-24.
- **Match ad mediation to the network you buy through** (single-mention): Mediation via AppLovin MAX gives more granular AppLovin UA data, while IronSource mediation favours IronSource buying (anecdotal). Choose the mediation layer that matches the network you actually spend on. Mobile-app context, not Roblox. Sources: [GameMakers 2024-10 28:37](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1717). Newest source 2024-10-24.
- **Adapt campaign mechanics when platform signals change instead of running unchanged setups** (one-creator-repeated): After iOS tracking changes (IDFA, 2023), advertisers who kept identical campaigns saw performance collapse. Do not give up on a channel entirely: iOS CPMs fell and competition thinned, so adapted setups could scale. A focused cross-functional team (engineering, data, UA) found workable tactics in a couple of weeks. Mobile-app context. Sources: [GameMakers 2023-05 2:02](https://youtu.be/dDV6SJodui8?t=122), [GameMakers 2023-05 2:02](https://youtu.be/dDV6SJodui8?t=122), [GameMakers 2023-05 4:04](https://youtu.be/dDV6SJodui8?t=244), [GameMakers 2024-10 12:52](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=772). Newest source 2024-10-24.
- **Measure creator marketing on performance, not vanity metrics** (single-mention): Creator marketing on PC and console is growing but measured poorly. Apply a performance mindset, track which creators drive installs or plays, and cut what does not work. Speaker is mobile-focused, so validate for Roblox. Sources: [GameMakers 2024-10 56:04](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=3364). Newest source 2024-10-24.

## Checklist
- [ ] Confirm retention and playtime are good before buying ads
- [ ] Check eligibility: 13+ account, ad account, payment method
- [ ] Use earned Robux (better rate) or ad credits; choose payment method before publishing
- [ ] Open Creator Hub > Ads and use the 3-step flow; start from recommended budget and duration
- [ ] Pick the objective: Maximize Plays for launch/testing
- [ ] Leave audience on All Players, all regions and devices at first
- [ ] Use a daily budget (~$5-20/day) and run 7-10+ days
- [ ] Prepare 2+ thumbnails/creatives and compare CTR (target ~2.5-3%+, min 1%)
- [ ] Check cost per play (~$0.008 target) on day one
- [ ] Don't restart or panic over low early stats or bot-like lobbies
- [ ] Monitor Acquisition analytics: home recommendation impressions vs sponsored impressions
- [ ] Stop ads once home recs exceed sponsored impressions
- [ ] If no progress after 3-5 days, improve onboarding/FTUE before spending more
- [ ] Re-advertise after big updates using Plays plus Reactivate (needs 20,000 lapsed players for Lapsed audience)
- [ ] Test search ads with relevant, high-volume keywords (max 10 per Ad Set)
- [ ] Use launch data for ad-specific onboarding or rewards
- [ ] Create separate share links per off-platform channel
- [ ] Use creator sponsorships matched to genre; check estimated views
- [ ] Post teasers and hype content before launches/events
- [ ] Disclose ads clearly; check AreAdsAllowed per player
- [ ] Register in-experience ad campaigns 5+ days ahead and submit assets for moderation
- [ ] Add rewarded video with clear reward text and multiple placements; track opt-in rate and frequency
- [ ] Call GetAdAvailabilityNowAsync right before showing the button
- [ ] Scale spend only while ROAS/retention holds (ROAS >100% for Earnings)
- [ ] Check the live Ads Manager UI for current settings; guides go stale
- [ ] Confirm the universe is eligible with campaign-options before creating any campaign.
- [ ] Set the ad goal to Maximize Plays for a new game, with all devices and regions unless you have a reason to narrow.
- [ ] Start with a small test budget and a 5-7 day run; reassess by about 2 weeks.
- [ ] After about one day, check CTR (aim roughly 2.5%+) and playthrough rate (aim over 1%).
- [ ] Pause any ad showing CTR near 0.3% with a play rate near 0.07.
- [ ] Judge the campaign by day-7 retention and sessions, not impressions or clicks.
- [ ] Calculate Robux returned against Robux spent on every campaign.
- [ ] Check daily that spend matches budget and that credits are not stopping early.
- [ ] Stop or pause the campaign once the engaged-player target is reached.
- [ ] Fix stability and retention before any paid spend.
- [ ] Send an idempotency key on every campaign creation request.
- [ ] Poll batchGetStatus until SERVING, NOT_SERVING or REJECTED.
- [ ] Use durationInDays for run length and keep it under 3650.
- [ ] Use PAUSED, not CANCELLED, for temporary stops.
- [ ] Raise budgets gradually because increases apply immediately.
- [ ] Upload creatives as Open Cloud image assets and reference them in creativeAssetIds (up to 10 per campaign).
- [ ] Before any ad spend, read Roblox's current ad documentation; the 2018 guides are dated.
- [ ] Confirm the experience's age rating allows ads (13+ audience floor; 17+ restriction reported in 2023, so verify).
- [ ] Write win criteria before launch: Robux beat, returning players, and ad payback at day 7.
- [ ] Decide product: Sponsored Items for Marketplace items; Ads Manager or the API for experiences.
- [ ] Finalise thumbnails and icons before launch; cancel and restart to change creatives on an active ad.
- [ ] Budget in ad credits (1 credit = 285 Robux) and convert all spend to Robux per play before judging.
- [ ] Check pacing and CCU within the first hour or two; lifetime budgets can burn out in hours.
- [ ] Measure CTR (below 1% suggests a weak thumbnail) and play-through (plays divided by impressions, aim 1.5-2%+).
- [ ] Compare paid players with home-recommendation players each day; reduce credits as organic players rise.
- [ ] Cancel early when results are clear to recover unused full days.
- [ ] Use Recent Players audience for win-back campaigns; use All Players for new acquisition.
- [ ] Promote to users over 16 to support Kids/Select evaluation.
- [ ] For group-owned games, check revenue under the group.
- [ ] Use a 7-day or 1-2 week run rather than a short burst, and re-run on major updates.
- [ ] Set in-experience ad fallback images and avoid clickable placements that send players away.
- [ ] Don't buy ads for a game the algorithm already surfaces, or one with unfinished core loop.
- [ ] Before buying credits, confirm game passes or dev products work and day-1 retention is healthy.
- [ ] Confirm the account is 13+ and verified before creating ads.
- [ ] In Ads Manager, pick the destination experience before submitting, since ads cannot be edited after.
- [ ] Set a custom daily budget and a fixed run length, and disable auto-reload.
- [ ] Set bid per play at roughly 0.02 for small budgets; never below 0.01.
- [ ] Exclude console in the ad set if you do not want console players.
- [ ] Start with US and Canada, then narrow age and gender to the audience that spends Robux.
- [ ] Target verified players and confirm clicks can actually enter the game.
- [ ] Schedule the run for your peak days (weekends or Friday to Sunday).
- [ ] Check delivery within about 30 minutes; zero impressions means investigate status.
- [ ] Use a proven thumbnail, with a cutout figure and play button for new variants.
- [ ] Let the campaign run continuously; avoid pulling and reposting it.
- [ ] Split large budgets across days and review between tranches.
- [ ] Track CTR, cost per play and joins per click, plus Robux returned, not just spend.
- [ ] Watch day-1 retention to confirm the ad promise matches the gameplay.
- [ ] Reconcile credits bought against credits spent, since unused credits are not refundable.
- [ ] Before enabling O18 targeting in paid UA, confirm the experience's age rating and Roblox audience rules allow it.
- [ ] Compare O18-segment day-7 retention and Robux per user against the all-player baseline before scaling spend.
- [ ] Check for in-game events, admin actions or promotions in the same window before attributing a traffic spike to ads.
- [ ] Track the share of total earnings coming from rewarded-video ads, not just ad spend, to judge monetization health.
- [ ] Before budgeting off-platform acquisition, check whether Roblox co-marketing or UA support programs apply to your game.
- [ ] When forecasting pre-roll ad revenue or reach, exclude Roblox Plus subscribers, since they won't see pre-roll ads.
- [ ] Confirm pre-roll status is still 'coming soon' or live in the current docs before promising creators revenue from it.
- [ ] Before buying cold traffic, plan for low click-to-play: a 2.5% CTR with about one play per three clicks means large impression volumes are needed.
- [ ] Create a separate lapsed-player campaign for existing games and measure the share of returners within 7 days.
- [ ] Record cost per activation for every campaign and compare it with Robux return before scaling.
- [ ] Judge each campaign over a multi-day window across repeated exposures, not on last-click conversions.
- [ ] For multi-game publishers, run separate audience and avatar tests per product before pooling budget.
- [ ] Use Discord quest buys for existing games to target lapsed players, and track the reactivation split against new users.
- [ ] Do not treat creator-driven download spikes as proof that paid ads are profitable.
- [ ] Launch each challenger creative in its own campaign; never add it to a champion campaign.
- [ ] Produce 20-50 new concepts per test cycle and size volume to monthly spend.
- [ ] Build a persona-by-trend matrix before producing creatives.
- [ ] Record CTR and IPM (impressions per install) for every creative.
- [ ] Check watch time and completion per placement, not only install rate.
- [ ] Validate any pattern from old campaign data in a controlled test before budgeting on it.
- [ ] Spend a small UA budget early to compare CPI with projected LTV and track retention.
- [ ] Re-check CPI vs LTV monthly, since CPI rises over time.
- [ ] Track churn per 1,000 impressions for players acquired through ads.
- [ ] Test a new country with a small paid campaign before influencers.
- [ ] Confirm influencer attribution method (link, code, or deep link) before paying.
- [ ] If the channel is TikTok, budget Spark Ads on top of the creator fee.
- [ ] Vet agencies with case studies before engaging them.
- [ ] Tell stakeholders the first influencer campaign is for learning.
- [ ] Keep influencer spend steady rather than cutting it abruptly.
- [ ] Make the game public before running the first ad.
- [ ] Launch the first campaign at 8 ad credits per day for 2 days with 2-3 thumbnails.
- [ ] Step to 16 ad credits per day for 2 days only if the first campaign shows traction.
- [ ] Stress-test ROAS targets against a 30% CPM drop.
- [ ] Expect paid installs to grow slower than impressions and wait for larger samples.
- [ ] Before launching any campaign, list all active campaigns and confirm none overlap unintentionally (N8106).
- [ ] Log CTR and QPTR together for every campaign. If CTR is healthy (around 2%+) but QPTR is under ~1%, stop adding budget and diagnose the first session (T18915, N8124, N8125).
- [ ] Do not schedule ads for launch day if you are still fixing bugs; ensure a stable build before traffic arrives (T18920).
- [ ] Start with a small first campaign to get early stats, then tune the game before raising spend (N8079, N8126, T18925).
- [ ] Check that the Robux spent is matched by retained players, not just CCU spikes (N8079).
- [ ] For mobile-app UA only: favour first-party ad platforms and limit DSP middlemen (T18786, T18787).
