- [ ] For mobile-app UA only: align mediation with the buying network (T18788).
- [ ] Measure creator marketing with install or play attribution, not reach alone (T18798).
- [ ] When a platform signal changes, rebuild campaign mechanics rather than rerunning the old setup (T18734).

## Where creators agree
- MoseyTube, Tizzy, Alex Builds Games, CallMeDogey and Deconstructor of Fun agree that ads seed the algorithm with data; stop them once organic home recs take over.
- DitchyDevelopments, kra_DeZ, Tizzy, CallMeDogey, AlvinBlox and Roblox official docs agree that ads cannot fix a bad game and that retention matters first.
- Roblox Creators, MoseyTube, Tizzy and CallMeDogey agree on broad targeting (All Players) for new games.
- Roblox official, MoseyTube, CallMeDogey and Tizzy agree on runs of about a week or more rather than 1-2 days.
- Roblox official and indies (Tizzy, SmartyRBX 2025, DevForum) agree that starting small and scaling after seeing results is better than heavy upfront spend.
- Roblox, SmartyRBX, TheDevKing and DevForum agree that off-platform promotion complements ads.
- DevForum authors and MoseyTube agree on CTR benchmarking and thumbnail A/B testing.
- Roblox docs and DevForum agree that rewarded video is 13+ only and that the ad button should be shown only when GetAdAvailabilityNowAsync confirms availability.
- Fix retention and stability before paying for ads (janisjanis, rasdev, Duckable, Yann).
- Test with small spend and check early metrics before scaling (janisjanis, rasdev, Yann).
- Ads do not raise organic ranking; they add players only (rasdev, with Roblox tests cited).
- Stop ads once engaged-player growth slows rather than running indefinitely (Yann, rasdev).
- Keep Ads Manager on Maximize Plays with broad targeting for new games (bubDEV, Yann).
- Ads alone rarely create retained players; retention, not impressions, decides payback.
- Good games convert cheap ad spend; bad games get few players even with spend.
- Stop or cut paid spend once organic home-recommendation players match or exceed ad-driven players.
- Run ads for about 1-2 weeks (or 7 days), not short bursts.
- Track ad return in Robux per play and day-7 retention, not spend or clicks alone.
- Creative quality (thumbnail, CTR) decides how much traffic the spend buys.
- Ads don't pay back immediately and revenue drops after spend ends (T15919, T16429, Q1583, T16208).
- Fix the game and retention before buying traffic (T16615, T16047, T16486, Q1611).
- Start with a small test budget and reassess before committing (T16050, T16364, T16483, T16205).
- Judge ads by CTR, cost per play and joins, not impressions (T16046, T16333, T16364).
- Thumbnails strongly affect CTR (T16712, N6589).
- Audience age and verification affect whether clicks convert to plays (T16533, T16534).
- Both MonarchDev and Deconstructor of Fun indicate that impressions and clicks alone do not show ad success; both point to downstream player conversion as the real measure.
- Test small before scaling: TheDevKing, janisjanis, GameMakers and Deconstructor of Fun all start with small spend and check results before committing bulk budget.
- CTR is the first filter for creatives: TheDevKing, janisjanis and GameMakers all rank ads on click-through rate.
- Judge acquisition on retention and quality, not raw installs: GameMakers and Deconstructor of Fun both stress retention, CPI vs LTV and churn.
- Creative volume matters: GameMakers and Deconstructor of Fun both show that winning ads come from producing many variations.
- janisjanis (T18803, T18925) and rasdev (T18953, T18954, Q1971) agree ads should seed early players, not rescue an unoptimised or weak game.
- janisjanis (T18915, N8125) and rasdev agree that ads should be judged by post-click quality (retention or play rate), not raw CTR or impressions.

## Where creators disagree
- **Daily vs lifetime budget**
  - CallMeDogey: always daily, never lifetime (steady flow signals consistency).
  - Roblox Creators (2025-06): daily budget with recommendations over lifetime.
  - waveshine (2024): lifetime budget gives waves of players and beats daily.
  - kra_DeZ (2026): lifetime budget in ad credits for thumbnail tests and main runs.
- **Whether ad traffic drives the algorithm**
  - MoseyTube, Tizzy, Alex Builds Games: ads give enough data for home recs.
  - Roblox official: ads are independent of RFY ranking; their users are excluded from RFY signals; ads add incremental players.
  - ItsZyonic (2026-09): ads alone didn't catch the algorithm; organic metrics climbed after he turned ads off, though he also said CCU dropped when ads went off.
  - Tizzy: sponsored traffic doesn't push you up on its own.
- **Importance of ads vs other promotion**
  - DevForum 2024: ads preferred over social/YouTuber outreach, whose spikes fade.
  - ItsZyonic 2023: avoid relying on ads, use sponsors; SmartyRBX and TheDevKing recommend creator sponsorships.
  - CallMeDogey 2026 and AlvinBlox: ads are a minor step, use sparingly.
  - DitchyDevelopments: ads are the fastest way to get players.
- **Campaign length and duration**
  - Roblox (2025-06): at least 1-2 weeks.
  - waveshine (2024): one-day ads.
  - kra_DeZ: 3 day test and 4-5 day main.
  - Tizzy: at least 3 days, ideally a week.
  - MoseyTube: ~10 days.
  - Alex: 7 days.
  - Older short-run advice is outdated by the official guidance.
- **Targeting age and settings**
  - ItsZyonic 2023: set sponsor age to 17+.
  - Tizzy 2026: all ages, no need for 16+ setting.
  - waveshine: all regions and ages unless blood/alcohol.
  - Roblox 2025: avoid manual stacking of targeting.
  - TheDankDeveloper: US/Canada only, console unchecked.
- **Best time to start ads**
  - MoseyTube: at launch, since Roblox needs data.
  - Tizzy 2026-07: about two weeks after launch.
  - Roblox: start small, fix UX from first players, then scale.
  - DitchyDevelopments and Tizzy: only with solid retention.
- **Rewarded video benchmarks**
  - Docs give 13%+ opt-in and 2.5+ frequency in one place, and 15%+ and 3+ in another, with 2.8 as an average.
- **Rewarded video eligibility**
  - Deconstructor of Fun and DevForum 2025-07: 100K DAU.
  - DevForum 2025-11 and docs: 2,000 unique monthly visitors; later update wins.
- **Typical budget size and units**
  - Erthlord (citing Bubix): about 50 ad credits per day as standard.
  - Yann: $15/day for 7-10 days for most genres; $50-100 total for a beginner.
  - Duckable: $16/day for 10 days, and says a huge budget is not needed.
  - bubDEV: lifetime 150-400 Robux-equivalent over 5-7 days.
  - Units differ (credits, Robux, USD), so figures are not directly comparable.
- **Run length**
  - bubDEV (Dec 2025): 5-7 days; longer runs are unnecessary.
  - Yann (May 2026): reassess by 2 weeks; under new eligibility rules it may be longer.
  - Existing knowledge file rule says run at least 1-2 weeks.
  - Yann (Jul 2026) says his 19-day run should have stopped sooner.
- **Lifetime versus daily budgets**
  - janisjanis (Dec 2024): avoid lifetime budgets, which burned credits quickly and delivered fewer players.
  - bubDEV (Dec 2025): lifetime budget with a 150-400 range works for new games.
  - Recent advice favours daily budgets, but lifetime still has defenders.
- **Whether to spend on ads before the game retains players**
  - rasdev (Apr 2026): seed the algorithm with small Robux ad spend early.
  - rasdev (Oct 2026): only pay for ads once the game already holds players.
  - Duckable (May 2026): spend only after the game retains players.
  - Newer rasdev advice (Oct 2026) supersedes the April seeding advice.
- **Whether ads affect ranking**
  - rasdev (Oct 2026): ads raise player counts but not ranking.
  - janisjanis (Sep 2025, Feb 2026): a campaign pushed the game into the algorithm.
  - Treat the algorithm-entry claim as self-reported and unverified.
- **Speed of results**
  - janisjanis: a scaled campaign produced a quick surge in players.
  - tef (Oct 2026): expect a slow start while the algorithm learns the audience.
- **Ads versus creators or organic**
  - janisjanis (Dec 2024): creator videos are the best way to gain players.
  - janisjanis (Jan 2026): paid ads likely beat organic TikTok unless videos reach millions of views.
- **Targeting narrowness**
  - bubDEV and Yann: keep all devices and regions to maximise reach.
  - janisjanis (Dec 2024): targeted US and Canada, mobile and PC, which produced the best ad.
- **Whether off-platform video (YouTube Shorts, TikTok) can replace paid Roblox ads**
  - Matt The Method: free YouTube Shorts drove the game's earnings with no ad spend (T13174).
  - Raul: free Shorts drove over 1,000 views but barely moved visits (max about 20 players); paid reach was needed (T13662).
  - VuukStudios: prefer Roblox ads over TikTok/Shorts for early traffic (T13822).
- **Whether thumbnails can be changed while an ad runs**
  - VuukStudios: swap thumbnails while ads run when CTR is below 1% (T13027).
  - Raul: Roblox does not allow a thumbnail swap on an active ad; cancel and restart (T13657).
- **Whether Roblox ads pay back**
  - Positive: Erthlord's campaign lifted players from 23 to 140 (T13150); Arow saw revenue jump after ads (T12759); VuukStudios says Roblox ads is king and a good game gets 10k players for 5k Robux (Q1438, Q1400).
  - Negative: Quez lost 4,964 Robux net (N4830); MonarchDev lost about $200 across two games (T13715); PineappleDev lost on 4,000 Robux (T12762); JustDucksey's ad players barely converted (T12575); BloxxedDev saw little return on $20 (T12244).
- **Whether ads are needed at all for a good game**
  - GnomeCode: focus on making a good game; the algorithm will pick it up (Q1280).
  - VuukStudios: the algorithm cannot pick you up if your game just sits (Q1421).
- **Campaign length**
  - VuukStudios and existing guidance: 1-2 weeks (N5452, T13835).
  - Raul and Arow: 7 days (T13658, T13401).
  - Short bursts of 2-3 days (BloxxedDev N4642, Quez N4822, PineappleDev N4589) produced visits but weak retention.
- **Audience targeting breadth**
  - Arow: All Players for acquisition, Recent Players for win-back (T13400).
  - Quez: narrowed to Indonesia and Malaysia, action genre (N4823).
  - Roblox Creator Docs: sponsored items require a target audience (T14835).
- **Bid level effect on placement**
  - JustDucksey: low bids (0.01 per play) push ads to less prominent placements (T12574).
  - Roblox Creator Docs: display share is proportional to daily budget relative to rival bidders; doubling a bid roughly doubles impressions versus a rival (T14834).
  - Note: mechanisms differ between experience ads and sponsored items.
- **Minimum worthwhile budget**
  - PolarBear: 70-100 Robux is enough to test (T16050); would not spend under 10,000 Robux on sponsoring (T16066, Q1522); only large budgets (~100,000) are worth it (T16067).
  - JustVeryYellow: 10k Robux is too small; 70k-100k more likely needed (T16334, Q1568).
  - Rapstrike: ~$130 over 10 days produced ~100 average CCU (T16726).
  - Note: these address different goals (test vs sustained growth) and different games.
- **Bid level per play**
  - PolarBear: ~0.02 typical, 0.05-0.06 for budgets above ~15,000 Robux (T16061, T16062).
  - BubuTheDev: 0.01-0.03 is enough; Roblox's suggested 0.04-0.25 is not necessary (T16145, Q1532).
- **Does paid traffic pay back?**
  - BubuTheDev: 70k Robux returned roughly 2x in game revenue before fees (T16136).
  - Mulch: 100k Robux returned 731 Robux in 2025 (T16208); net loss ~66k over 19 months (N6249).
  - ThePurpleGuy: expect a loss on immediate Robux; treat as exposure (T16429, Q1583).
  - PolarBear: not worth it at ~5,000 Robux (T16066).
- **Campaign objective**
  - Existing rule: set the goal to Maximize Plays.
  - JustVeryYellow: Maximize place visits (T16331).
  - LovJak: engagement objective targeting all players (T16636, T16544).
  - RoyalDev: engagement or audience targeting; budget and start time barely matter (T16593).
- **Daily vs lifetime budget**
  - BubuTheDev: daily cap prevents credits being spent quickly; lifetime spreads the full credit across the duration (T16142).
  - JustVeryYellow: uses lifetime budget with a set duration (T16331).
  - RoyalDev: daily vs lifetime and start time barely matter (T16593).
- **Breadth of targeting**
  - Existing rule: broad targeting and let Ads Manager find players.
  - BubuTheDev: narrow to US and Canada, then to the spending age and gender (T16143, T16144).
  - Lnor: narrowing to verified 18-24 PC and mobile raised CTR from ~1.5% to 8%+ (T16534).
- **When to judge or kill an ad**
  - Existing rule: kill weak ads early using CTR and play-rate thresholds.
  - RoyalDev: do not judge after 2-3 days of on/off cycling; that produced the worst results (T16595).
- **Audience targeting approach**
  - Alex Builds Games: vary audience and avatar per product in a media-buying style to find winners (T17905).
  - Existing knowledge file rule: use broad targeting and let Ads Manager find players. Source not named in this batch.
- **Whether the second campaign improved on the first**
  - MonarchDev: second campaign had higher CTR than the first, with no exact figures (T17853, N7632). Cited as evidence for testing creatives, not for a specific target.
- **How much to polish creatives**
  - GameMakers (T18440): test fast without over-polishing; a wacky ad can beat a careful one.
  - Existing file rule: finalise thumbnails and icons before launch (treat as the older, more conservative position).
- **How many creatives to test**
  - GameMakers (N7880, T18433): produce 20-50 concepts per winner.
  - Deconstructor of Fun (N7981): smaller publishers under $1M/quarter struggle to keep up with top spenders' creative volume, so tiny teams should aim lower.
  - janisjanis (T18625): a few sequential campaigns, stopping weak ones, was enough for a small game.
- **Audience versus messaging segmentation**
  - Existing file rule: use broad targeting and let Ads Manager find players.
  - GameMakers (T18435): segment by motivation persona so each creative speaks to one motivation.
  - janisjanis (T18709): all-player audience for the first campaign. These are compatible: persona segmentation happens in creative, while audience stays broad.
- **Campaign goal naming (Roblox)**
  - Existing file rule: set the ad goal to Maximize Plays.
  - janisjanis (T18709, Aug 2026): goal named 'Players'. Likely a UI label change; verify in Ads Manager before relying on either name.
- **Stop after seeding versus always-on channels**
  - Existing file rule: use ads to feed the algorithm, then stop.
  - Deconstructor of Fun (T18667): keep influencer marketing as a permanent pillar. These address different channels, so both can hold.

## Date context (what may be outdated)
- 2018 official ads advice (Robux budgets like 25,000/day or 500 start) predates the modern Ads Manager.
- 2020 DevForum advice (1% min / 3% CTR, 3-way split, 50k-100k Robux front-page budget) is old; CTR benchmarks persist in 2023 and 2026 (2.5%) posts.
- Sponsored tiles were consolidated into one sort and lost effectiveness (2022); the homepage sponsored row is dynamic (rows 2-10) as of 2025-06.
- 2024 waveshine / DevForum low-bid (0.01-0.02) and lifetime-budget advice predates the 2025 three-step Ads Manager redesign (40 clicks to 3; Plays/Retention/Reactivate; Earnings objective is newer). waveshine himself says settings change.
- Roblox's 2025-06 guidance (1-2 weeks minimum, daily budget, minimal targeting) supersedes 1-day or lifetime tactics.
- Tizzy's mid-2026 $16/day for ~11 days reflects a 'threshold change'; earlier hits cost ~$100-200.
- Rewarded video eligibility dropped from 100K DAU (mid-2025) to 2,000 monthly visitors (Nov 2025) and creator age from 18+ to 13+; Deconstructor's 2025 statements are outdated.
- Revenue share for brand campaigns starts Jan 1, 2027 per docs. Docs-based items are dated 2026-10, so they are the most recent.
- Campaign goal was 'Maximize plays only' in the Feb 2026 transcript (N949) while other sources list four goals/Earnings; check the live UI.
- SmartyRBX's 2023 ad pessimism was later superseded by his 2025 use of small seed budgets.
- Aug 2026: Kids and Select engaged-player threshold reportedly fell from 500 to 250 (rasdev). Older advice citing 500 is outdated.
- Under the new eligibility requirements, the 2-week maximum run guidance from May 2026 (Yann) may no longer apply.
- Oct 2026: Roblox Creator Docs cover the campaigns API, including eligibility checks, IN_REVIEW status, durationInDays and budget timing. Older Ads Manager-only advice may not reflect these.
- Oct 2026: the engagement-based ad goal is in beta and can overspend; check its current state before relying on it.
- 2023-2024 figures (janisjanis, Robux-era settings and CTR) predate current eligibility and pricing changes and should be treated as historical.
- Budget units changed over time: credits, Robux and USD appear side by side in sources. Convert carefully before comparing.
- Lifetime budgets were criticised in Dec 2024 but recommended for new games in Dec 2025; daily budgets are the recent default.
- 2018 DevForum guidance (T10507) predates the rebuilt ad system; read current Roblox docs first.
- 2020 scheduled ads (T10847, N4208) said a 28-day ad needs no daily bid. The 2026 Creator Docs (N5844) set a 10 Robux/day minimum for sponsored items; treat 2020 wording as superseded.
- 2020 refund rule (N4211) matches 2026 Creator Docs (N5846); still current.
- 2023 restrictions (T11739 on 17+ titles, N4477 on the over-13 floor) and the 2023 in-game advert price (N4464, 2 Robux) are dated; verify before relying on them.
- Budgets have risen. 2024: 10,000 Robux/day (N4566). 2025: about 1,000 Robux/day to start (N4859). 2026: about 45-50 ad credits/day (about 12,800-14,300 Robux) per campaign (N5193, N5076), or 30k-60k Robux total over 1-2 weeks (N5401). The existing ~$10/day rule is outdated for 2026 and likely undersized for scaled campaigns.
- Credit units shifted: ad credits (1 credit = 285 Robux) have been used since 2024; 2026 sources quote both credits and Robux, so convert consistently.
- Pre-roll video ads were 'coming soon' as of Sept 2026 (T13814); check whether they have rolled out.
- Sponsored Items docs (Oct 2026) and the limited-time test budget (Oct 2026) are the newest official material.
- Robux-era sponsored experiences (2021 to 2024-04) used Robux bids and budgets; from 2024-03 spending moved to ad credits (~250-285 Robux per credit depending on purchase).
- Sponsorship access path: Creator Hub, Manage Experiences, Dashboard, then the Sponsor button, then Ads Manager (2024-03).
- Creating ads requires 13+ and verification (2025-03). The European 13+ rule was reported in 2025-11.
- Ad CTR figures improved with targeting: 2021 broad all-platform ads ~0.019%; 2024 ~0.18% to 0.95%; 2025 ~5.69%; 2026 ~2% to 3.6%, and Lnor 1.5% to 8%+ after targeting. Treat older low CTR as a pre-targeting benchmark.
- Most recent (2026) guidance: engagement objective, continuous running, ~24-hour learning, 500 engaged-player reach gate, auto-reload off. Where this conflicts with older Maximize Plays advice, the 2026 advice is more recent; verify in current Ads Manager.
- Platform-invited sponsored events (2021) are not a standard self-serve option now.
- Roblox Investor Relations figures dated 2026-09 are the newest official data here; they add scale context and do not change existing budget or bidding rules.
- The Speed Keyboard Escape figures are from Aug 2026 and come from a single self-reported agency source.
- Pre-roll video ads were described as 'coming soon' in the official DevForum post dated 2026-09; check current docs for launch status and rev-share terms.
- Co-marketing and UA support programs were reported in 2026-03 (GamesBeat, self-reported); program terms may have changed since.
- Brand activation count (600+) is as of Q4 2025, so it is already dated by the knowledge cutoff of June 2026.
- Newest items are from October 2026 (Deconstructor of Fun). Lapsed-player reactivation and multi-touch attribution are the most recent emphasis; they add to, but do not overturn, earlier advice.
- MonarchDev's CTR and click-to-play figures come from an August 2026 first campaign after launch. Later campaigns may perform differently, and no follow-up figures are provided.
- No conflicts with the existing file's Robux-era or 2025-era guidance were found in this batch; the existing 'Recent Players for lapsed ones' rule is consistent with the new reactivation data.
- TheDevKing's 2020 figures (100k Robux, tablet/phone/desktop CTRs) are Robux-era and predate the current ad-credit system; use them only as historical baselines.
- GameMakers' 2021-22 data covers Facebook, Google, TikTok and Snap, plus IDFA-era iOS constraints (IDFA margin claim, ~500 iOS games per month). Treat these as pre-ATT context, not current Roblox benchmarks.
- janisjanis' Nov 2025-Aug 2026 campaigns use ad credits and the 8-then-16 credits-per-day ladder. For 2026 budgets, prefer these over older Robux-denominated budgets.
- Deconstructor of Fun's Feb and Jun 2026 notes are the most recent: creative volume benchmarks, TikTok Spark Ads cost structure and influencer organic-uplift figures. Re-check these as of 2026-11-01, since platform costs change.
- The Facebook and Audience Network figures (N7873, N7878) are off-platform and not directly transferable to Roblox ads.
- GameMakers items (2023-05 and 2024-10) cover mobile-app UA after iOS tracking changes and DSP and mediation, not Roblox. Treat them as analogies only.
- janisjanis figures (2024-10 to 2025-10) reflect a period when Roblox classic and new ad systems coexisted (N8107), so CTR benchmarks may differ by system.
- rasdev (2026-06) is the most recent Roblox-specific advice here and aligns with the existing seed-then-organic guidance; prefer it where older advice conflicts.
- No conflicting advice was found between the new items and existing rules.

## Instructions for an agent using this file
- If the user asks whether to run ads on a new game, first check retention and the first-time experience; if weak, advise fixing them before spending.
- If advising a first-time budget, suggest about $10/day for ~10 days (range $5-20/day), Maximize Plays, All Players, all regions and devices; label dollar figures as self-reported indie claims.
- If the user asks when to stop, say stop once home recommendation impressions exceed sponsored impressions in Acquisition analytics, and don't restart repeatedly (rolling 7-day window).
- If the user proposes a 1-2 day campaign, cite Roblox's guidance of at least 1-2 weeks, with 3-5 days minimum before judging results.
- If CTR is below 1%, replace the creative; target 2.5-3%+. Run thumbnail A/B tests before large spend. For daily vs lifetime, recommend daily (official + CallMeDogey) and note waveshine and kra_DeZ use lifetime.
- If the game is old or lapsed after a major update, recommend Maximize Plays + Reactivate Users for 1-2 weeks; check the 20,000 lapsed / 10,000 recent minimums.
- If asked about search ads, advise relevant high-volume keywords, max 10 per Ad Set, single-keyword Ad Sets for comparison, and no audience targeting.
- If the user wants rewarded video, check eligibility (13+, ID, 2FA, public unrestricted game, 2,000 monthly visitors, maturity questionnaire), call GetAdAvailabilityNowAsync just before display, show clear non-random non-Robux rewards, and track opt-in (13-15%+) and frequency (2.5-3+).
- If ads are placed in an experience, check AreAdsAllowed per player, add disclosure labels, register campaigns 5+ days ahead and obey billboard size limits; for brand deals recommend in-game integrations over standalone games plus legal review.
- If indie claims conflict with official docs on mechanics, prefer official docs and the most recent date, and tell the user to check the live Ads Manager UI since settings change.
- If the user asks to start or fund a sponsored campaign, first confirm eligibility via campaign-options and check that the game holds players and has no stability problems.
- If a campaign shows high impressions but low plays or CTR near 0.3%, recommend pausing and fixing the game before spending more.
- If a campaign is performing, recommend scaling in small tranches and measuring each before the next.
- When recommending budgets, state the unit (Robux, ad credits or USD) and the genre and explain the range.
- Use day-7 retention and sessions as the success metric, not impressions or clicks.
- When writing API code, include an idempotency key, poll status, use durationInDays and batch status checks, and use PAUSED rather than CANCELLED for temporary stops.
- Prefer the Oct 2026 official docs and the 250 Kids/Select threshold over older figures; mark indie numbers as self-reported.
- If a user is planning a large spend, recommend tranches and a return calculation (Robux returned versus spent) before committing.
- If an experience is 17+ rated, don't plan ad-driven acquisition without first checking current Roblox rules.
- If asked about cost, convert all credits to Robux at 285 per credit and state the Robux cost per play.
- If a user is advertising a marketplace item, use Sponsored Items (min 10 Robux/day, 24h-28 day cycle); otherwise use Ads Manager or the Advertising API.
- If home-recommendation players exceed ad-driven players, recommend reducing or stopping paid credits.
- Before recommending spend, confirm the core loop and retention are working; if not, advise fixing them first.
- When a user needs to change a thumbnail mid-campaign, say the active ad must be cancelled and restarted (one creator; verify).
- Compute refund for early cancel as full days remaining only, not partial days.
- Treat indie numbers as self-reported and flag low-advice items; prefer official 2026 docs over 2020-2025 creator posts.
- Flag unit mismatches between sources (credits vs Robux vs USD) instead of mixing them.
- Pre-roll video ads need no code; don't write custom ad code for them.
- If the game has no working monetisation or weak day-1 retention, advise against paid spend until those are fixed.
- If a user asks for a budget, give test and scale ranges separately (e.g. ~100 Robux test vs 10k-100k scale-up), noting that creators disagree.
- Before recommending an objective, ask whether the goal is testing or sustained growth; 2026 creators favour engagement objectives.
- Always recommend a custom daily budget and disabling auto-reload.
- If an ad shows zero impressions after ~30 minutes, check campaign status and eligibility before concluding it failed.
- Advise against pulling and reposting campaigns; judge results after the learning period and at least 1-2 weeks, unless thresholds clearly fail.
- If CTR is high but day-1 retention or plays are low, diagnose audience and messaging mismatch first.
- Convert credits to Robux using the actual purchase price (about 250-285 Robux each), not a single assumed rate.
- Treat indie ROI claims as self-reported; do not present them as official Roblox figures.
- For European or under-13 audiences, check the 13+ and verification requirements before planning.
- If a campaign's audience is allowed to be 18+ and the goal is retention or monetization, test an O18 segment against all players before scaling.
- If a traffic or CCU spike coincides with an event or admin action, do not credit it to ads until the event is ruled out.
- When citing rewarded-video revenue or sponsored-ad prevalence, use the official Sep 2026 IR figures and note that the ad-prevalence figure is correlational.
- If asked about co-marketing or UA funding from Roblox, cite T17596 and label it as indie-reported, not official.
- If asked about pre-roll reach or revenue, state that Roblox Plus subscribers won't see pre-roll ads (T17663).
- If asked about the size of Roblox brand activations, cite N7475 with its Q4 2025 date.
- Do not treat the 2026-09 pre-roll 'coming soon' status as launched without checking current official docs.
- If a campaign targets lapsed players, then report the share of players who return within 7 days alongside cost per activation.
- If cost per activation is above Robux return, then pause the campaign rather than scaling.
- If a player appears after several ad impressions but no click, then credit the ad campaign on a multi-day window, not on the last click.
- If running Discord quest buys for an existing game, then split reporting between new users and lapsed reengagement.
- If a publisher runs several games, then test audience and avatar combinations per product before pooling budget.
- If a creator activation drives a download spike, then do not attribute that spike to paid ads without a separate test.
- If CTR is high but plays are low, then review the landing experience and the ad promise before increasing budget.
- If a creative's CTR is below your kill threshold, stop it. Compare IPM and watch time before scaling any survivor.
- If you need a new creative pipeline, plan 20-50 concepts per cycle, sized to monthly spend, and build them from a persona-by-trend matrix.
- If you want to test a challenger against a champion, launch it in its own campaign; never add it to the champion's campaign.
- If you plan to enter a new country, run a small paid campaign there first and scale only when retention, CPI or ROI look good.
- If CPI exceeds the LTV you forecast, pause scaling and fix retention or monetisation before adding spend.
- If ad-sourced players churn at high rates per 1,000 impressions (100 per 1,000 is very bad), treat the creative or audience as a quality problem, not a volume problem.
- If the influencer channel is TikTok and you need trackable installs, add Spark Ads cost (about double spend) to your budget or choose another channel.
- If you are funding influencers for the first time, set expectations that the first campaigns are for learning and that cutting spend later increases install decline.
- If the first 8-credit-per-day, 2-day campaign shows traction, step to 16 credits per day for 2 days; do not jump straight to full budget.
- Use ad credits, not Robux, when budgeting 2026 campaigns; treat 2020-22 Robux and Facebook numbers as historical.
- If a campaign's CTR is healthy but QPTR is under ~1%, pause budget increases and diagnose the first session before scaling.
- If the user asks whether ads can fix a weak or unoptimised game, advise against it; ads should only seed initial players.
- If a launch-day bug is still open, delay campaigns until the build is stable.
- Before launch, check that no unintended campaigns are running concurrently.
- Treat mobile-app UA items (DSP, mediation, iOS IDFA) as off-Roblox; apply them only when the user asks about non-Roblox apps.
- Quote the source (indie, self-reported) when citing janisjanis, GameMakers or rasdev figures.

## Not covered here (research elsewhere)
- No verified data on conversion of ad cohorts into revenue by genre; most outcomes are unverified indie claims.
- Little detail on Ads Manager creative specs (image sizes, video formats, thumbnail rules) for sponsored ads.
- No guidance on how Robux-vs-USD ad-credit exchange rates change over time or tax/fee implications.
- Lacks details on Earnings objective setup (ROAS tracking, minimum economy size) and the New Users objective.
- Actual rewarded video EPM/CPM numbers are absent.
- No coverage of influencer pricing, contracts or disclosure requirements for off-platform promotion.
- Limited evidence on Discord, community building and notifications beyond a brief note.
- Unclear current status of the under-18 creator ad restriction; verify in current docs.
- Unclear whether Tizzy's home-rec 'threshold change' is documented officially.
- No official guidance on recommended budgets, run lengths or ROAS targets; figures are indie self-reports.
- No official definition of an engaged player or of the Kids and Select qualification rules beyond the 250 threshold.
- No official data on attribution windows, how visits are counted, or how ad credits convert to Robux.
- No official creative best practices beyond thumbnail count and asset format.
- No coverage of sponsored placement mechanics, pricing, or auction dynamics.
- No data on ads for non-game brands or sponsorships priced in dollars.
- No information on how the engagement-goal beta behaves after it leaves beta.
- No benchmarks for CTR by genre or for cost per acquisition in USD.
- No coverage of Ads Manager UI steps, account billing setup, or policy review timelines beyond the IN_REVIEW status.
- Little evidence on long-term retention from ad-acquired players beyond janisjanis (Dec 2024) and rasdev (Oct 2026).
- No official ROAS, CPM or CTR benchmarks; all figures are indie and self-reported.
- No current confirmation whether the 17+ ad restriction still applies in 2026.
- No details on pre-roll rollout dates, eligibility or revenue share.
- No official data on retention benchmarks (day-1/day-7) for ad-acquired players.
- No detail on Ads API endpoints, rate limits or sponsored-item reporting fields.
- No information on in-experience Ad GUI revenue share or eCPM.
- No guidance on how group ownership changes ad billing or permissions beyond one creator's note.
- Exchange rates for Robux and ad credits vary over time; no official conversion schedule was found.
- Little data on how ads affect Developer Products or Game Pass conversion beyond Arow's single example.
- No [OFFICIAL] Roblox documentation in this batch; bid floors, credit pricing, reach thresholds (500 engaged players) and the ~24-hour learning period are self-reported and need checking against current docs.
- No controlled A/B tests; samples are small and mostly single-game, so benchmarks are not generalisable.
- Fees, DevEx conversion and net return are rarely accounted for; ROI claims are mostly gross Robux.
- No data on the In-Experience ad placement beyond a 'test separately' note.
- No details on rewarded video or in-experience ad monetisation metrics beyond Cumin's fandom-wiki observation (half-screen video ads, one ~40 minutes long), which is an unverified anecdote.
- No coverage of off-platform promotion, creator sponsorships or group-owned game ad revenue in this batch.
- Unknown how the platform defines 'highly engaged players' and whether the 500 threshold is official or stable.
- No post-2026 pricing or policy information; check current Ads Manager and advertise.roblox.com before quoting numbers.
- No ROAS, CPM, CPP or cost-per-install benchmarks in these additions.
- No mechanics detail on how O18 targeting works in Ads Manager or the API, or how it interacts with age-rating rules.
- The Speed Keyboard Escape metric definition (CCU, visits or sessions) is not stated.
- No methodology for the Roblox IR figures (sample, time window, definition of rewarded-video games).
- No evidence on whether sponsored ads cause higher earnings for top games or only correlate with them.
- Revenue-share percentage and payout terms for pre-roll ads are not given in the sources.
- Eligibility, application process, and budget size for Roblox co-marketing and UA support programs are not covered.
- The sources do not explain how the 7 billion visits figure is measured or whether it applies to brand activations specifically.
- No ROAS or CPI benchmarks from the new sources; check other sources for current acquisition cost figures.
- No independent verification of the 50% lapsed-player return rate or the under-$2 cost per activation; both are creator-reported.
- No benchmark CPM, CPC, or cost-per-play figures for comparison.
- No definition of ROAS, the attribution window, or the payback period used by the creators.
- No sample sizes or statistical confidence for the MonarchDev campaign figures.
- No official Roblox guidance on lapsed-player audiences or Discord quest buy mechanics in this batch.
- No data on how avatar or audience variation affects results across different genres.
- No OFFICIAL Roblox documentation appears in this batch; Roblox-specific creative test benchmarks (CTR by genre, IPM, CPI, ROAS) are thin and mostly indie self-reports.
- No statistical guidance for creative tests: sample size, significance thresholds or how long to run a test before deciding.
- No Roblox-specific watch-time or completion data for pre-roll or rewarded placements.
- No outcome numbers for Discord quest ad buys beyond the existing rule.
- No influencer rate cards or creator-discovery guidance specific to Roblox.
- No conversion rate or fees between Robux, Wallet and ad credits, or agency fee benchmarks.
- Current Apple and Google UA policy and ATT effects in 2026 are not covered; the IDFA figures are 2022 claims.
- No information on how the ad-credit ladder behaves beyond 16 credits per day or over longer campaign lengths.
- No official Roblox documentation in these items for the current ad system, billing, or policy changes in 2026.
- No Roblox-specific CPI, ROAS, or Robux-per-play benchmarks beyond one indie's single-game data.
- Little on how classic and new ad systems differ in practice, beyond one CTR comparison.
- Mobile UA advice (DSP, mediation, iOS) does not translate directly to Roblox sponsored experiences; Roblox-specific creator-marketing data is missing.
- Sample sizes are small and self-reported; no statistical validation of the thresholds (CTR, QPTR) given.

- "you're better off finding content creators who can make a video on your game this will be the best way to gain players" ([janisjanis01 2024-12 9:29](https://youtu.be/9EXrUN7PUrE?t=569))
- "the objective was to get visits not awareness" ([JustDucksey 2025-02 0:44](https://youtu.be/nQPm7gmRBzs?t=44))

- "we definitely got cooked on this one... we're going to try to make it easier. We're going to add more levels so the player is engaged" ([Quez 2025-03 4:33](https://youtu.be/QPxXr57M-xM?t=273))
- "We made 166 Robux. So, if we do the calculations here, we only lost 4,964 Robux." ([Quez 2025-03 4:00](https://youtu.be/QPxXr57M-xM?t=240))
- "there is almost no other place that advertisers can advertise to get to this audience. So the CPMs are going to be likely a little bit higher." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2025-04 7:43](https://youtu.be/v_0RADBCTlw?t=463))
- "for the first week, I would do about 1,000 Robux a day, just to get your game in the algorithm" ([Arow 2025-05 2:03](https://youtu.be/_fVivJ4DtsA?t=123))
- "I spent about 4,000 Robux to advertise this game, which is not a lot at all." ([PineappleDev 2025-05 4:07](https://youtu.be/o4dJZ-UqznE?t=247))
- "Running for at least one to two weeks allows Roblox to optimize, get you the right players." ([Roblox Creators 2025-06 14:40](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=880))
- "Our creators actually like need less targeting because the Roblox ads manager is really helping you find the best users." ([Roblox Creators 2025-06 36:18](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=2178))
- "not only have I lost my time and labor, but 99,269 Robux." ([Mulch 2025-06 9:35](https://youtu.be/B2vC4-QArWg?t=575))
- "Eligible creators earn Robux for every ad shown within their experience, and early tests have shown that users who view ads spend more Robux within experiences." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-07 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/more-creators-can-now-use-rewarded-video-ads/3838678))

- "50 ad credits for like 7 days... 50 ad credits is pretty standard." ([Erthlord 2025-08 7:03](https://youtu.be/dB6Us5PaWFw?t=423))
- "If you do a standalone game, you may or may not get any visits at all. You're not going to get meaningful reach from a brand game with two or ten concurrent users." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2025-09 29:14](https://youtu.be/8pruyZlxMI4?t=1754))
- "You have to have a 100,000 DAUs on your game or experience to even apply to do rewarded video." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2025-09 10:19](https://youtu.be/LRyThu3bFyg?t=619))

- "Ads are not meant to fix this trend." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-retention-by-acquisition-source-and-select-your-benchmark-set/4010157))
- "As long as the experience has no free-form user creation or AI interaction, any public, unrestricted experience with over 2,000 unique monthly visitors can integrate Rewarded Video." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-11 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rewarded-video-ads-are-now-available-to-all-ads-eligible-creators/4063278))
- "Monetization from advertising is not currently factored into the Home Recommendations algorithm." ([Roblox DevForum 2025-11 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rewarded-video-ads-are-now-available-to-all-ads-eligible-creators/4063278))
- "What I learned was it takes a lot of Robux to make a game popular. I don't think 10k is enough." ([JustVeryYellow 2025-11 11:23](https://youtu.be/rzB8CcRzFK8?t=683))
- "Don't trust anyone that tells you that there's some hidden configuration that if you don't give them 50% of your game, you won't be able to set yourself." ([bubDEV 2025-12 6:12](https://youtu.be/7z8rfg_jBuk?t=372))
- "if you're advertising your own game, you don't really need to put that much in ads to start." ([ItsZyonic 2026-01 5:13](https://youtu.be/4-ge-E9WvM4?t=313))

- "Ad networks are optimizing for CPM, not ARPDAU... they don't pay that retention tax. The publisher does." ([Deconstructor of Fun 2026-02 9:51](https://youtu.be/Z_UOQ2nSZq4?t=591))

- "Do commissions, get money for ads." ([Tizzy 2026-06 12:05](https://youtu.be/Rkgbcv6omPg?t=725))
- "Roblox has no idea if your game is good yet. So, it's just not going to push it to anybody at all, and ads are basically just how you give Roblox enough data" ([MoseyTube 2026-06 0:19](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=19))
- "The algorithm already evaluated it once and this run is basically just showing it the updated version." ([MoseyTube 2026-06 3:16](https://youtu.be/esZQX5COI_E?t=196))

- "Stop wasting money on ads to fix a game problem." ([rasdev 2026-07 2:43](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=163))

- "It's essentially me spending money on the endless void of what Roblox calls an ad system." ([MonarchDev 2026-08 5:02](https://youtu.be/MANBsA1MTzI?t=302))
- "Ads only scale what's already working." ([RoyalDev 2026-08 1:22](https://youtu.be/6CefFrCFqcI?t=82))

- "It was almost a no brainer for us to keep pushing forward with ads." ([Roblox DevForum 2026-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ads-manager-rdc-recap-earnings-roas-reporting-incrementality/4909790))
- "I generally lowered the ads ad credits amounts when the home recommendations number went higher than the ad credits number." ([Seibo 2026-10 0:43](https://youtu.be/FmAD02FS_3k?t=43))

- "the Roblox ad algorithm is learning who wants to play this type of game. And then it's going to start pushing out to like my real target audience." ([tef 2026-10 14:28](https://youtu.be/OfObWnrucRY?t=868))
- "Eligibility does not guarantee Roblox will serve ads in your game, and nothing obligates Roblox to serve ads into your game." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads))
- "weave the brand into your game's core loop" ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/monetization))
- "Qualified visits are billed once per session; engaging 10 branded assets in one session counts as 1 qualified visit." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations))
- "Campaign eligibility is determined by the most restrictive asset label applied to any of your registered assets." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations))
- "Learn how to increase earnings with rewarded video and get inspiration from top games in your genre." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/rewarded-video-guides))
- "Engagement, retention, and monetization from users acquired through ads are excluded from RFY ranking signals." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/discovery-faq))
- "Search ads differ from other ads in that they also calculate the relevance of the game to the user's query. This means that games that are more relevant to a search query have a greater chance of displaying as ads." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/search-ads))
- "At this time, search ad and sponsored ad results are combined as a single Sponsored Ad metric on acquisition reports." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/search-ads))
- "The two metrics that you need to focus on are opt-in rate and daily frequency." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/incremental-sim))
- "Rewarded video should act as the edge a player reaches for right before a match, or the lifeline they grab right after a loss, never a distraction from the action." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/sports))
- "In an RPG, an ad is not an interruption; it is a micro-transaction paid for with time." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/rpg))
- "Rewards for video ads cannot be randomized items." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads))
- "Issue the reward to the user immediately after the rewarded video ad has finished playing." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads))

## Verified additions

_Hand-added, labelled and sourced rows kept in `youtube-learn/supplements/topics/ads-and-sponsored-experiences.md`; they survive rebuilds._

- **Current Ads Manager objectives are Plays, Earnings (Limited) and Engagement** (official): Docs fetched 2026-11-01 list objectives Plays (players likely to start a session), Earnings (likely to spend Robux; limited availability) and Engagement (age-checked highly engaged players; smaller audience, usually higher cost per play). Audiences: All, New (never played or 180+ days), Recent (last 30 days, needs 10,000+ players), Lapsed (30-180 days, needs 20,000+). The 2025-08 DevForum names (Acquire New Users, Drive Retention, Reactivate) are superseded by this scheme; confirm in the live dashboard. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-11](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager), [Roblox DevForum 2025-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/3862159). Newest source 2026-11-01.
- **Learning period: plan for 24-48 hours and do not pause** (official): The docs page says the first 24 hours of an active campaign are Learning; Roblox staff (RDC recap) say 24-48 hours and that it restarts on each pause. Treat 48 hours as the safe window. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-11](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager), [Roblox DevForum 2026-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ads-manager-rdc-recap-earnings-roas-reporting-incrementality/4909790). Newest source 2026-11-01.
- **Official average cost per play benchmarks** (official): DevForum staff 2025-08-05 gave Maximize Plays $0.008, Drive Retention $0.007, Reactivate $0.011. Auto-bidding means you set budget and duration, Roblox sets the bid. The docs page itself publishes no CPP, CTR or ROAS target values. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/3862159), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-11](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager). Newest source 2026-11-01.
- **Rewarded video has no published EPM; eligibility is now 2,000 monthly uniques** (official): EPM = Robux earned per 1,000 impressions and earnings = EPM x impressions, but neither the docs (2026-11-01) nor the launch thread publish a value or an earnings example. Launch (2025-07/08) required 100K+ DAU; docs now require 2,000 average monthly unique visitors, a 13+ publisher with 2FA and ID verification, and rewards must be developer products worth about 3-10 Robux. Roblox expects 85%+ completion, up to 3% of earnings in the first months and 5-10% revenue growth from ads products long term; no opt-in benchmark is published. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-11](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads), [Roblox DevForum 2025-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/more-creators-can-now-use-rewarded-video-ads/3838678). Newest source 2026-11-01.
- **Immersive ad EPM anecdote is old and small-sample** (single-mention): ZeeZee (2023) saw 617 impressions and 86 Robux in 3 days on a hobby game, reading EPM as 140 Robux per 1,000 and later about 50. Immersive ads, not rewarded video; do not use as a current rate. Sources: [ZeeZee 2023-04 2:45](https://youtu.be/Vi3Ck32SuiY?t=165). Newest source 2023-04.
- **Ad testing heuristics from med_v3 (ungraded creator)** (single-mention): Start with 20+ ad credits and never test below 15; about 3% CTR and about 2.25% qualified play-through per user are called good; aim ads at the game's main gender and 18+ unless built for teens. Consistent with other CTR sources but the creator is not graded in creator-credibility. Sources: [med_v3 2025-03 2:31](https://youtu.be/7ZKZo0enAMU?t=151). Newest source 2025-03.
- **Influencer and creator deal pricing is not published** (single-mention): A 2026-11-01 search found no price list; DevForum recruitment threads show pay negotiated per channel size, in Robux, admin or in-game perks. Treat any quoted rate as anecdote and contact creators directly. Newest source 2026-11-01.
- **17+ experiences CAN be advertised, but only to users 17+** (official, 2026-11-01): The 2023 'could not use Immersive Ads/User Ads/Sponsored Experiences' note is superseded: since 2025-01-31 staff allow Sponsored Experiences and Search Ads for restricted (17+) and paid-access experiences, which cannot serve ads to ages 13-17. Ads Manager needs age 13+ and a verified email (no ID check for advertisers); ad publishers (experiences showing ads) need 18+ and ID verification. Sources: [DevForum 2025-01-31](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/advertising-paid-access-restricted-17-experiences/3432252), [Roblox Creator Docs ads-manager 2026-11-01](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager). Newest source 2025-01-31.
- **Kids/Select Highly Engaged Player (HEP) thresholds: entry 250, maintenance 25; older 500/100 figures are superseded** (official, 2026-11-01): Docs say a game completes evaluation at 250 unique plays by highly engaged age-checked users in a 60-day window. Staff cut entry 500 -> 250 on 2026-08-19 and maintenance 100 -> 25 on 2026-07-16. Creators who said 500 (Tizzy, LovJak, VuukStudios) or 'below 100 loses eligibility' were right for their date only. RDC26 staff post announces a further drop to 100 in Nov 2026 (announced, unconfirmed live). Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs kids-and-select 2026-11-01](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/kids-and-select), [DevForum staff 2026-08-19](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/highly-engaged-player-threshold-drops-to-250/4820164). Newest source 2026-08-19.
- **Expedited review costs 50,000 Robux per game (not 100,000)** (official, 2026-11-01): Current docs: one-time, refundable, auto-refunded 90 days after the game becomes eligible (or 90 days after payment if never eligible), forfeited only if permanently removed for Community Standards violations. 100,000 was the June 2026 launch price (creator Yann 'I would have paid the 100,000 Robux fee' is the older price). Review takes up to 48h; minor violations restart the clock. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs kids-and-select 2026-11-01](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/kids-and-select), [DevForum staff 2026-08-19](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/highly-engaged-player-threshold-drops-to-250/4820164), [DevForum Kids/Select launch 2026-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-kids-and-select-global-launch-upcoming-updates-to-eligibility-ads-manager-and-expedited-review/4685717). Newest source 2026-11-01.

## Appendix: every extracted tip on this topic

- 2018-07-24 Roblox [5:11](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=311): Run ads consistently for a week at a fixed daily budget (~25,000 Robux/day); a test showed ~100-150% more revenue than the no-ad week.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [3:48](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=228): Optimise both ad CTR (ad click) and the second CTR (game page click to Play); clickbait ads may get high first CTR but low Play rate.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [11:59](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=719): Sponsored games place your game on the front page; sponsored listings hide the like/dislike ratio, useful after a rough beta.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [25:37](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=1537): Start with small ad budgets (~500 Robux) and reinvest returns to scale up gradually.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [36:39](https://youtu.be/JTV8apW2oBk?t=2199): Combine ads, sponsored placements, YouTube and Twitter together; small channels add up to a big impact.
- 2018-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-resources-mega-thread/177780): Read 'The Economic Guide to Profitable Game Advertising' and 'Using the Roblox Advertising System' before spending on ads.
- 2020-05-07 TheDevKing [8:37](https://youtu.be/AZTDrfuk9ME?t=517): Don't rely only on Roblox sponsored ads; pay YouTubers or influencers to play your game, since viewers copy what they watch.
- 2020-05-07 TheDevKing [17:44](https://youtu.be/AZTDrfuk9ME?t=1064): Split sponsor budget by platform: target mobile-heavy audiences for mobile games and PC for PC; speaker says ~50k Robux can get a good game to the front page.
- 2020-05-27 TheDevKing [5:35](https://youtu.be/sBOAlER6HVU?t=335): Test sponsored ads with a small budget (e.g. 10k Robux) first to check CTR before committing the bulk of the spend.
- 2020-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805): Do not run any ad that falls below 1.0% CTR; aim for about 3.0% CTR.
- 2020-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805): Decide first whether you want a small dedicated community (40-90 players) or a wider discovery audience (100-1,000+) before choosing ad types.
- 2020-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805): Launch test: split budget into three equal ads (clickbait, community, mobile sponsor), then put daily spend on the highest-CTR one for three days; archive the second-best.
- 2020-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805): After launch, keep running smaller ads on the archived second-best channel, funded by game revenue, to maintain a playerbase of 500+.
- 2020-10-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/we%E2%80%99ve-rebuilt-sponsored-games/832597): Use the new scheduled ads: one ad can run up to 28 days, so you no longer need to set a bid for every day it runs.
- 2020-10-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/we%E2%80%99ve-rebuilt-sponsored-games/832597): Use the Sponsor button next to your game on the Creations page to create new sponsored ads in the rebuilt system.
- 2020-10-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/we%E2%80%99ve-rebuilt-sponsored-games/832597): Use gender and basic age targeting to aim ads at the audience most likely to play your game.
- 2020-10-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/we%E2%80%99ve-rebuilt-sponsored-games/832597): If you stop an ad early, Roblox refunds each full day the ad did not run, so stop promptly to recover unused Robux.
- 2021-08-11 SmartyRBX [0:24](https://youtu.be/Y5lmDjab25E?t=24): Promote on social media, since no successful game skipped it; TikTok and Instagram had little competition at the time.
- 2021-08-27 Last Level Studios [12:24](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=744): Roblox approached the creator for sponsored events (Fruity Pebbles, Imagination); being placed in platform events drove large prize-claim numbers.
- 2021-09-05 Mulch [1:34](https://youtu.be/yG44d_Xnbwg?t=94): Sponsored experiences ran about 33,333 Robux/day; a broad all-platform, all-ages ad was seen by 280.3M people but got only ~0.019% CTR.
- 2021-09-05 Mulch [2:55](https://youtu.be/yG44d_Xnbwg?t=175): Sponsored ad cost about 5 Robux per play (cost per click ~1.86 Robux, ~0.008% post-view conversion). Check whether these benchmarks fit your goal before spending.
- 2021-09-05 Mulch [9:19](https://youtu.be/yG44d_Xnbwg?t=559): Revenue drops quickly once a paid ad ends; organic traffic must be sustained to keep sales up after the campaign.
- 2021-11-10 GameMakers [12:45](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=765): Check placement-level watch time and completion, not just install rate: a placement's skip rules determine how long viewers actually see the ad.
- 2021-11-10 GameMakers [26:44](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=1604): Use legacy campaign data to find patterns, then validate them in a controlled test before relying on them for budget decisions.
- 2021-11-10 GameMakers [39:05](https://youtu.be/Ga7g6ZJhFfo?t=2345): Use learned placement and duration sweet spots to rule out placements that will not work, and to prioritise creative production for the rest.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [25:15](https://youtu.be/DHvfEB8myyU?t=1515): Use sponsored ad targeting (gender, age, platform) to learn which audiences engage most, since every engagement also feeds organic ranking.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [25:55](https://youtu.be/DHvfEB8myyU?t=1555): Sponsored ads run 1 to 28 days with a daily Robux budget that acts as the bid; the budget largely determines impression share.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [21:33](https://youtu.be/DHvfEB8myyU?t=1293): Compared with mobile stores where cost per install is about $2 to $3.50, Roblox sponsored plays cost pennies, making paid discovery cheap to test.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [14:48](https://youtu.be/bKvOPfp3oX0?t=888): Release teaser trailers on YouTube and share them on Twitter; reveal the story but not the event itself, and end each trailer with date and time.
- 2022-03-01 GameMakers [0:03](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=3): Expect most creatives to fail: 85-95% of new concepts fail to beat the best ad, so produce 20-50 concepts to find one winner.
- 2022-03-01 GameMakers [5:58](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=358): Build a matrix: player motivations (rows, from personas) crossed with competitive creative trends (columns). Each cell is a concept with a defined intent.
- 2022-03-01 GameMakers [11:52](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=712): Segment target players into personas by motivation (e.g. excitement, success, relaxation) so ads speak to a specific motivation, not a vague one.
- 2022-03-01 GameMakers [26:58](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=1618): Test fast and iterate without over-polishing. Judge each ad on click-through rate and impressions per install (IPM); a wacky ad can beat a careful one.
- 2022-03-01 GameMakers [33:52](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=2032): Test on Android on Facebook first, since iOS creative testing became harder. Keep a winning ad in its own campaign; do not mix challengers into it, because the champion's spend history makes challengers lose.
- 2022-03-01 GameMakers [33:52](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=2032): Size creative volume to monthly spend velocity: too many creatives for the budget wastes them, too few causes fatigue and running out.
- 2022-05-12 Roblox Creators [12:12](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=732): Brand IP lowers user acquisition costs because audiences already have affinity; retention still depends on the game being fun and authentic to the brand.
- 2022-05-19 Roblox Creators [0:47](https://youtu.be/HxqWoF_wfMQ?t=47): Sponsored tiles lost effectiveness after Roblox put them in one sort instead of scattering them; use them more diligently rather than heavily.
- 2022-08-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-art-of-robux-getting/1922578): Research Roblox ad systems for your genre and run analytics on every ad campaign before launching a game, clothing group, or UGC.
- 2022-08-30 GameMakers [38:20](https://youtu.be/JVCao-Yhu2M?t=2300): Understand UA economics: CPI rises over time due to audience saturation, new competing apps and competitors' improving KPIs, so standing still erodes profitability.
- 2023-01-19 Roblox Creators [0:00](https://youtu.be/YdplC2WVkWc?t=0): Consider a paid access period only if the game offers unique value; Emergency Response charged 300 then 50, then 25 Robux during early access before going free.
- 2023-01-29 SmartyRBX [2:25](https://youtu.be/DOMuVNlHv8Y?t=145): If running ads, aim for a high CTR and monetisation good enough that ad spend at least breaks even.
- 2023-02-08 SmartyRBX [0:45](https://youtu.be/rdWyuDEpGyk?t=45): Market the game actively: make strong ads and use Roblox sponsored ads and normal ads, rather than relying on the game being good alone.
- 2023-02-08 SmartyRBX [0:45](https://youtu.be/rdWyuDEpGyk?t=45): Reach out to Roblox YouTubers and use TikTok to spread the word about the game.
- 2023-02-08 SmartyRBX [0:45](https://youtu.be/rdWyuDEpGyk?t=45): Create promo codes and promote them on social channels to drive more people into the game.
- 2023-02-23 Roblox Creators [34:23](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=2063): Track whether players actually click key buttons by tying a badge to the click; a hacky but practical analytics method.
- 2023-04-27 Roblox Creators [0:05](https://youtu.be/vNKPlYeXj0Q?t=5): Prerelease hype is a cheap way to promote; build it with speculation content rather than paid spend.
- 2023-05-25 GameMakers [2:02](https://youtu.be/dDV6SJodui8?t=122): Build a cross-disciplinary team (engineering, data, UA expertise) to find post-IDFA workarounds; a couple weeks of focused work can yield viable iOS tactics.
- 2023-05-25 GameMakers [2:02](https://youtu.be/dDV6SJodui8?t=122): Don't drop iOS entirely; iOS CPMs are lower now and competition is thinner, so advertisers who adapt can scale profitably.
- 2023-05-25 GameMakers [4:04](https://youtu.be/dDV6SJodui8?t=244): Expect that advertisers who ran unchanged campaigns saw plummeting performance; adjust mechanics rather than keep identical setups.
- 2023-06-20 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/create-experiences-for-people-17-and-older-on-roblox/2432184): 17+ experiences cannot use Immersive Ads, User Ads or Sponsored Experiences at launch; plan monetisation without ad-driven acquisition for 17+ titles.
- 2023-06-29 ItsZyonic [1:37](https://youtu.be/ODh2jPEGWUc?t=97): Don't depend on Roblox ads; competition makes them inefficient for small developers. Prefer sponsors, which are newer and give more control.
- 2023-06-29 ItsZyonic [2:17](https://youtu.be/ODh2jPEGWUc?t=137): Set sponsor age targeting to 17+ for best return regardless of audience; choose phone or PC based on target audience.
- 2023-07-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-is-coming-to-meta-quest/2465291): Test your experience on Meta Quest before enabling the VR checkbox, since Roblox auto-enabled VR for some default-script experiences and you can uncheck it on the Access page.
- 2023-08-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279): Target ads to players aged 13+, since Roblox only shows ads to that age group; match the game's audience.
- 2023-08-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279): Aim for ad CTR of at least 1.0%, ideally above 3.0%; stop underperforming ads quickly and try new ones.
- 2023-08-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279): Split ad budget across variants, measure CTR, then shift most spend to the best performer while keeping some testing.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [13:16](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=796): TikTok can drive large reach even without a creator following; short clips of the game got millions of views despite ~300 views per post.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [15:34](https://youtu.be/XJLW1G1Z-8A?t=934): Until brand suitability controls ship, only all-ages rated experiences can host immersive ads; wait for those controls if rated higher.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [3:22](https://youtu.be/Z-ZKqBPdl44?t=202): Mobile paid UA is costly (~$2.20 per install); on Roblox, organic traffic lets small creators compete if retention, engagement and monetization are strong.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [14:12](https://youtu.be/yUDvI8mGnn8?t=852): Put portals in your experience: one publisher reported up to 27% of monetization coming from portals.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [12:36](https://youtu.be/yUDvI8mGnn8?t=756): A lightweight branded trailer-style experience can be built in a week or two and is a viable path for brand campaigns like movie or product launches.
- 2023-10-23 janisjanis01 [0:25](https://youtu.be/5E5pj7CB4mM?t=25): Sponsored ads at 333 Robux/day gave 7,146 impressions, 81 clicks, 16 visits over 3 days. Test small spend before committing large budgets.
- 2024-01-22 VuukStudios [5:10](https://youtu.be/a3w0s1fBg5M?t=310): Advertise your services in Roblox developer Discord servers and reach out directly; one student received 2-3 client DMs per day from this.
- 2024-02-07 PolarBear [2:06](https://youtu.be/tNkQSpq2SXg?t=126): Judge sponsored ad results by clicks and players, not impressions; here ~8.1K impressions yielded only 15 clicks (~0.18% CTR) and 3 players.
- 2024-02-07 PolarBear [0:00](https://youtu.be/tNkQSpq2SXg?t=0): Test ad spend on a game with real content and monetisation in place first; the creator ran ads on a joke game with no game passes at launch.
- 2024-02-08 PolarBear [2:45](https://youtu.be/SNauF9OES7E?t=165): Make a simple ad image in Canva using a recognisable figure with background removed (remove.bg) and a play button; takes about a minute.
- 2024-02-08 PolarBear [2:45](https://youtu.be/SNauF9OES7E?t=165): Pick an audience-matching face: the speaker targets fans of a specific artist or creator whose audience overlaps the game's genre.
- 2024-02-08 PolarBear [4:46](https://youtu.be/SNauF9OES7E?t=286): Start with a small ad budget around 70-100 Robux to test; the speaker used 79 Robux and called 100 the practical minimum.
- 2024-02-20 ByteBlox [0:24](https://youtu.be/okGvZjneq60?t=24): Add an Ad GUI inside a part, scale the part, then pick the face, adornee, and enable video ads. Roblox supplies the ad content and pays you.
- 2024-02-20 ByteBlox [2:12](https://youtu.be/okGvZjneq60?t=132): Set a fallback image on the Ad GUI so the slot still shows something when no ad loads (server or client issues).
- 2024-02-20 ByteBlox [2:54](https://youtu.be/okGvZjneq60?t=174): Avoid giant billboard ads at spawn; they feel spammy. Weave ads into gameplay surfaces, like in-world screens, where players see them without it hurting the experience.
- 2024-02-20 ByteBlox [3:36](https://youtu.be/okGvZjneq60?t=216): Only add ads once the game has a meaningful number of players; ads earn little with no traffic, so monetise after you have an audience.
- 2024-02-20 ByteBlox [4:18](https://youtu.be/okGvZjneq60?t=258): Place ads where players will look at them but not feel them as intrusive; if an ad is clickable, avoid accidental clicks that send players to unrelated experiences.
- 2024-02-28 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/discovery-on-roblox-past-present-and-future-vision/2859111): Use on-platform ads, community, friends and off-platform influencers to grow the initial audience before the algorithm assesses you.
- 2024-03-01 MonzterDEV [11:41](https://youtu.be/em15yK5RjBk?t=701): Grow an initial audience first (small ads buy, friends, influencers, TikTok/YouTube) so the algorithm has signal to start recommending the game.
- 2024-03-14 1Coal [4:50](https://youtu.be/gWNOANEPYLY?t=290): Paid ads alone plateaued (about $50/day gave the same players); pair them with organic virality.
- 2024-03-19 PineappleDev [2:55](https://youtu.be/NB8XNNDdSjQ?t=175): Convert ad credits to Robux before judging cost: at 1 credit = 285 Robux, 0.04 credits per play ≈ 11 Robux per play. Compute real cost-per-play in Robux.
- 2024-03-19 PineappleDev [1:26](https://youtu.be/NB8XNNDdSjQ?t=86): Ads Manager offers a sponsored-experience placement alongside the in-list placement; the sponsored option showed a much larger target audience. Test it rather than leaving defaults.
- 2024-03-19 PineappleDev [4:58](https://youtu.be/NB8XNNDdSjQ?t=298): Paid traffic alone may not pay back; check hourly revenue in Overview against ad spend before scaling campaigns.
- 2024-03-25 PolarBear [1:51](https://youtu.be/bwMUqu-wWpw?t=111): Sponsoring runs via Ads Manager: buy ad credits with Robux (1 credit = 285 Robux here), then create a campaign with a date range and budget.
- 2024-03-25 PolarBear [3:17](https://youtu.be/bwMUqu-wWpw?t=197): Set a daily budget and schedule when creating the campaign; the creator didn't spend all credits, leaving about half unused (unused credits can't be refunded to Robux).
- 2024-03-28 PolarBear [2:26](https://youtu.be/sk8HQDgJyE0?t=146): Turn off console targeting in the ad set if you don't want console players; the speaker removed console to narrow the audience.
- 2024-03-28 PolarBear [3:36](https://youtu.be/sk8HQDgJyE0?t=216): Sponsored Experience placement is the one the speaker recommends; the In-Experience option (ads inside other games) is a test to try separately.
- 2024-03-28 PolarBear [3:36](https://youtu.be/sk8HQDgJyE0?t=216): Bid is cost-per-play; bids around 0.02 ad credits per play were used. Below 0.01 won't show. Higher bids win better auction placement.
- 2024-03-28 PolarBear [4:16](https://youtu.be/sk8HQDgJyE0?t=256): Budget bid tier by spend: about 0.03-0.02 for smaller budgets, 0.05-0.06 if investing over roughly 15,000 Robux.
- 2024-03-28 PolarBear [5:06](https://youtu.be/sk8HQDgJyE0?t=306): Choose the destination experience carefully at campaign creation; the speaker notes ads cannot be modified after submission.
- 2024-03-28 PolarBear [6:29](https://youtu.be/sk8HQDgJyE0?t=389): Schedule sponsored campaigns for weekends when more players are online, per the speaker's reasoning.
- 2024-03-28 PolarBear [0:00](https://youtu.be/sk8HQDgJyE0?t=0): Access sponsoring via Creator Hub: Manage Experiences, Dashboard, scroll down, click Sponsor. Then go to Ads Manager to create a campaign.
- 2024-04-13 PolarBear [0:40](https://youtu.be/Hh31hjo3J6U?t=40): Sponsored ads at this scale were not worth it: ~5,000 Robux yielded 376 plays. Creator would not spend under 10,000 Robux on sponsoring.
- 2024-04-13 PolarBear [2:05](https://youtu.be/Hh31hjo3J6U?t=125): If sponsoring, consider only large budgets (~100,000 Robux per his comment); otherwise spend on other ad options he suggests.
- 2024-06-02 SmartyRBX [1:46](https://youtu.be/0jUL69PGeV4?t=106): Pick creators whose audience matches your genre; a shooter should not be sponsored by a Roblox roleplay YouTuber.
- 2024-06-02 SmartyRBX [2:32](https://youtu.be/0jUL69PGeV4?t=152): Check the estimated views shown before sending an offer; more projected views means more promotion and more players.
- 2024-06-02 SmartyRBX [2:32](https://youtu.be/0jUL69PGeV4?t=152): Set a video due date and add notes/questions in the offer so the creator's requirements are clear before they accept.
- 2024-06-02 SmartyRBX [3:14](https://youtu.be/0jUL69PGeV4?t=194): Attach a UGC item to the offer if you have one so the creator promotes it alongside the game.
- 2024-06-02 SmartyRBX [4:34](https://youtu.be/0jUL69PGeV4?t=274): Use the rewards panel to earn extra platform credits by doing more sponsorships; new accounts start with $5 credit.
- 2024-06-02 SmartyRBX [5:10](https://youtu.be/0jUL69PGeV4?t=310): Post a public sponsorship listing so creators apply to you, choosing platforms (YouTube, Shorts, TikTok), a due date and a budget (minimum $15 in the example).
- 2024-06-16 ItsZyonic [4:42](https://youtu.be/b7qEitHbhUA?t=282): Minimum Roblox ad spend is 2,800 Robux per game; the creator used it as a starting push, reporting ~1.5% CTR after a day.
- 2024-07-06 TheDevBacon [2:51](https://youtu.be/_XJVDRr2ZuA?t=171): Roblox sponsored ads are the last option; the speaker says they don't work well.
- 2024-07-16 TheDankDeveloper [8:16](https://youtu.be/maRjVb1NAYc?t=496): Ads brought about 1,600 visits and ran about a day; 400+ concurrent players showed up at peak, but traffic did not convert to spend.
- 2024-07-30 ItsZyonic [3:01](https://youtu.be/Cd6mpD4doGs?t=181): Test sponsored experience vs in-game portal ads on small budgets before scaling; the creator planned to compare but did not report the result.
- 2024-07-30 ItsZyonic [3:01](https://youtu.be/Cd6mpD4doGs?t=181): Verify audience targeting: the sponsored audience skewed under-18 with many players lying about age, so ad spend reached the wrong buyers.
- 2024-08-04 BloxxedDev [0:00](https://youtu.be/fDJoaxTuRf4?t=0): Short Roblox ad bursts spike traffic fast but die off within minutes; most impressions arrive at the start of the campaign.
- 2024-08-04 BloxxedDev [1:16](https://youtu.be/fDJoaxTuRf4?t=76): Track ad spend against Robux earned, not just visits; here $20 in ads yielded only 105 Robux, so measure ROAS before scaling.
- 2024-08-07 Roblox Creators [19:32](https://youtu.be/2t_zhxyLOJ8?t=1172): Run a pre-launch countdown: post a daily short teaser across nine social accounts, and pair launch with a UGC item event from a partner.
- 2024-08-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-make-a-popular-game/3115791): Prefer Roblox ad campaigns over social media or YouTuber outreach for release; the author says influencer spikes fade within a day or two.
- 2024-08-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-make-a-popular-game/3115791): Spend about 10,000 R$ on sponsorship spread slowly over 10-15 days so the algorithm can pick the game up.
- 2024-08-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-make-a-popular-game/3115791): Set a low max bid (author cites 0.02 at time of writing); Roblox refunds unspent campaign budget.
- 2024-08-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-make-a-popular-game/3115791): Advertise heavily in the first three days to learn which device and age group engages, then target that audience.
- 2024-08-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-make-a-popular-game/3115791): Sponsor in 2-4 day intervals; if metrics are still bad by day 5-7, consider abandoning the project.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [5:22](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=322): Moving sponsored experiences higher on home drove 5x impressions with engagement constant, so more bid inventory means cheaper acquisition.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [6:43](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=403): Try search ads: users acquired via search often beat sponsored-experience users on engagement and monetization. Bid on unique title keywords.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [8:05](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=485): Check keyword volume with Audience Estimator before bidding; favour high-volume keywords few other creators bid on, since ads are auctioned.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [23:15](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=1395): Use ads at key moments like Halloween to re-engage old users and drive day-5 and day-7 return, not just day-1 installs.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [30:03](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=1803): Split-test immersive ads before full rollout; Vex saw revenue uptick with neutral or improved player metrics.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [25:05](https://youtu.be/QC6XWgJRJEM?t=1505): Pair a big update with a large ad spend to boost early algorithm pool prioritization and recommendations.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [31:00](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=1860): Regional targeting is limited to country level in ads; for sub-country targeting, partner with creators whose audience matches the region.
- 2024-10-24 GameMakers [25:11](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1511): Master a few first-party channels (Google, Meta, TikTok, Snapchat, Apple Search) and rewarded networks rather than chasing new ad networks.
- 2024-10-24 GameMakers [26:35](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1595): Cut or limit DSP middlemen; their margin (reported 20-40%) is hard to justify against leveraging first-party algorithms directly.
- 2024-10-24 GameMakers [28:37](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=1717): Match ad mediation to buying network: mediation via AppLovin MAX gives better AppLovin UA data granularity, and IronSource mediation favors IronSource buying (anecdotal).
- 2024-10-24 GameMakers [56:04](https://youtu.be/uhj_oQDScy0?t=3364): Creator marketing on PC and console is growing but measured poorly; apply a performance mindset and track what actually works.
- 2024-10-27 janisjanis01 [5:59](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=359): Don't blow a big ad budget in one go; he spent 10,000 Robux for ~1,000 CCU that left quickly. Run several smaller ads, raising spend gradually, and measure retention.
- 2024-10-28 waveshine [3:21](https://youtu.be/3BHo5a_OF5U?t=201): Use a lifetime budget rather than daily; speaker says lifetime delivers waves of players and beats daily.
- 2024-10-28 waveshine [3:21](https://youtu.be/3BHo5a_OF5U?t=201): Set the ad to run exactly one day; the speaker has not tested other durations.
- 2024-10-28 waveshine [4:02](https://youtu.be/3BHo5a_OF5U?t=242): In the ad set, pick Sponsored Experience, all regions and ages (unless blood/alcohol), and enable only devices your game truly supports, such as console.
- 2024-10-28 waveshine [4:02](https://youtu.be/3BHo5a_OF5U?t=242): Bid around 0.01 to 0.02 per the speaker; he says most people bid wrong here.
- 2024-10-28 waveshine [4:44](https://youtu.be/3BHo5a_OF5U?t=284): Ad settings change over time; test budgets, bids and durations yourself rather than copying a guide.
- 2024-11-07 GnomeCode [11:05](https://youtu.be/g9UbIrR0RbU?t=665): Don't spend on Roblox ads if your game is already well known to the algorithm; returns were poor (~2 cents per play) for a saturated, low-monetised game.
- 2024-11-07 GnomeCode [11:46](https://youtu.be/g9UbIrR0RbU?t=706): Use ad spend on brand-new games with zero players: the algorithm doesn't know them yet, so an ad burst gives initial CCU, playtest data and exposure to help recommendations.
- 2024-11-07 GnomeCode [3:05](https://youtu.be/g9UbIrR0RbU?t=185): Ad manager lifetime budgets can burn out in hours rather than spreading over the scheduled window; check pacing early.
- 2024-12-03 BubuTheDev [0:47](https://youtu.be/82HrjFTdPkU?t=47): Ads can pay back: creator says 70K Robux spend returned roughly 2x in game revenue, though fees were not subtracted.
- 2024-12-08 janisjanis01 [4:45](https://youtu.be/9EXrUN7PUrE?t=285): Watch CTR early; a 0.3% click-through rate and 0.07 play rate signalled a failing ad that wasn't worth continuing.
- 2024-12-08 janisjanis01 [4:45](https://youtu.be/9EXrUN7PUrE?t=285): Avoid lifetime budgets for sponsored ads in this creator's test; they burned credits quickly and delivered fewer players.
- 2024-12-08 janisjanis01 [7:49](https://youtu.be/9EXrUN7PUrE?t=469): Expect sponsored traffic to be mostly new Roblox users who barely play games; most left quickly, so retention will be poor.
- 2025-01-15 CallMeDogey [1:54](https://youtu.be/USJC5l_wpuY?t=114): He spent ~$40 on ads over two months, reached ~9,000 visits, and reinvested earned Robux into ads, which triggered growth.
- 2025-01-19 janisjanis01 [5:26](https://youtu.be/hxjumUFspP0?t=326): Be wary of paid ads: about 100,000 Robux spent on ads was called the biggest waste of the budget.
