- 2025-02-04 GameMakers [12:12](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=732): Spend a small UA budget early to measure the CPI vs LTV gap, and track retention at the same time.
- 2025-02-21 BubuTheDev [0:00](https://youtu.be/T3la9iXIGHQ?t=0): Ad Manager budget of ~5,700 Robux was quoted as reaching about 3.2 million people; the creator reported zero impressions after 30 minutes, so check delivery status before assuming the ad works.
- 2025-02-21 JustDucksey [0:44](https://youtu.be/nQPm7gmRBzs?t=44): Ad credits minimum buy was 10 (2,850 Robux); daily budget minimum is 5. Campaign objective was visits, not awareness.
- 2025-02-21 JustDucksey [2:06](https://youtu.be/nQPm7gmRBzs?t=126): Low bids (0.01 per play) push ads to less prominent placements; higher bids get better placement but cost more per play.
- 2025-02-21 JustDucksey [5:36](https://youtu.be/nQPm7gmRBzs?t=336): Ad traffic brings many joiners but most leave quickly; retention and monetisation of paid-acquired players was poor here, so ads alone did not convert to Robux.
- 2025-03-16 BubuTheDev [0:41](https://youtu.be/0NK_JBYEILA?t=41): Use daily budget cap if you don't want credits spent quickly; lifetime budget spreads the full credit across the whole campaign duration.
- 2025-03-16 BubuTheDev [1:22](https://youtu.be/0NK_JBYEILA?t=82): Start audience targeting with US and Canada; the speaker reports these work well for his ads, but test to confirm for your game.
- 2025-03-16 BubuTheDev [1:22](https://youtu.be/0NK_JBYEILA?t=82): Narrow gender and age targeting to match your spending audience (e.g. 13-17 or 18-24) based on who actually spends Robux in your game.
- 2025-03-16 BubuTheDev [2:04](https://youtu.be/0NK_JBYEILA?t=124): Bid low per-game (around 0.01 to 0.03 Robux) to avoid overspending; the speaker says Roblox's suggested 0.04-0.25 range is not necessary.
- 2025-03-31 Quez [0:28](https://youtu.be/QPxXr57M-xM?t=28): Sponsored ads at ~$0.14 CPM-equivalent cost (~5 Robux/1000 impressions) with 5% CTR still lost money; ads didn't pay back. Test small before scaling.
- 2025-03-31 Quez [4:00](https://youtu.be/QPxXr57M-xM?t=240): Track cost per play (here 2.85 Robux) and compare against retention, not just CTR; cheap clicks don't offset short sessions.
- 2025-04-04 AlvinBlox [4:02](https://youtu.be/f63stwpsaXw?t=242): Use Roblox ads sparingly; a simple small ad is fine, but don't spend heavily on them.
- 2025-04-08 Deconstructor of Fun [4:09](https://youtu.be/v_0RADBCTlw?t=249): Only developers aged 18+ can put ads in their experiences; under-18 creators cannot monetise games with ads under current rules.
- 2025-04-08 Deconstructor of Fun [4:52](https://youtu.be/v_0RADBCTlw?t=292): Expect integration difficulty: speaker says the low sophistication of Roblox developers makes ad integration a real obstacle.
- 2025-04-19 JustDucksey [3:31](https://youtu.be/qxE7JhikvwY?t=211): Reinvesting early Robux earnings into ads for a small game is a way to test whether it can attract players.
- 2025-05-02 Duckable [10:44](https://youtu.be/Wc8wjiy1oYk?t=644): Test paid ads with a small budget first; the speaker spent $113 on ads for 4 days and got 74 active players, so set a budget you can afford to lose.
- 2025-05-18 Arow [2:03](https://youtu.be/_fVivJ4DtsA?t=123): Start ads at about 1,000 Robux/day for the first week to get the game into the algorithm, then scale to 5,000–10,000 Robux/day once metrics are green/yellow.
- 2025-05-18 Arow [1:15](https://youtu.be/_fVivJ4DtsA?t=75): Track Developer Products revenue before and after turning on ads; the speaker saw revenue jump once ads started and kept climbing.
- 2025-05-21 PineappleDev [4:07](https://youtu.be/o4dJZ-UqznE?t=247): Spending 4,000 Robux on ads for ~3,000 visits and 0 active players lost money; validate retention before scaling ad spend.
- 2025-06-07 AlvinBlox [9:48](https://youtu.be/Y93DRfB-Deo?t=588): Spend small, steady Robux over a long period rather than one large burst; large spend is not required.
- 2025-06-09 Roblox Creators [10:48](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=648): Start with the new Ads Manager flow: three steps (goal, budget, creative) instead of the old ~40 clicks; accept recommended budget and duration first.
- 2025-06-09 Roblox Creators [14:40](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=880): Run campaigns at least one to two weeks; one- to two-day campaigns don't give the system enough time to optimize for the right players.
- 2025-06-09 Roblox Creators [14:40](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=880): Use daily budget with recommendations rather than lifetime budget; run-continuously works as an always-on traffic stream until ad credits run out.
- 2025-06-09 Roblox Creators [28:42](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=1722): Split budget across objectives in a funnel, e.g. ~60% Maximize Plays, ~20% Drive Retention, ~20% Reactivate Users, as one example split.
- 2025-06-09 Roblox Creators [30:04](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=1804): Use Maximize Plays for new launches, mid-life games, or updates; use Drive Retention for players who played in the last 0-30 days.
- 2025-06-09 Roblox Creators [31:26](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=1886): For a major update on an old game, run Maximize Plays and Reactivate Users together in a one-to-two-week flight to reach both new and lapsed players.
- 2025-06-09 Roblox Creators [36:18](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=2178): Avoid stacking manual targeting (gender, age, country); it shrinks audience size. Keep targeting open at first and test geo or device guard rails separately.
- 2025-06-09 Roblox Creators [38:19](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=2299): Run an A/B test of no-targeting in the new flow against targeted campaigns in the classic flow, comparing cost per play, before fully committing.
- 2025-06-09 Roblox Creators [23:53](https://youtu.be/D_YmdVZxIZw?t=1433): Homepage sponsored row is now dynamic (row 2 to row 10, previously fixed at row 5); engaging games rank higher, so retention and engagement matter.
- 2025-06-22 SmartyRBX [9:32](https://youtu.be/BMS5iYw_8Vk?t=572): Advertise early with a paid promotion budget and keep adding guns and gamepasses; the game began drawing players organically afterward.
- 2025-06-26 Mulch [0:41](https://youtu.be/B2vC4-QArWg?t=41): Split large ad budgets across multiple days rather than spending it all in one day; spread spend to test performance over time.
- 2025-06-26 Mulch [9:35](https://youtu.be/B2vC4-QArWg?t=575): Paid ads alone did not convert: 100k Robux spend yielded 731 Robux revenue. Validate monetisation and retention before scaling ad spend.
- 2025-06-28 SmartyRBX [5:11](https://youtu.be/Lplz7ZFcKlQ?t=311): Seed a new simple game with a small ad budget (he used 1,000 Robux) and keep advertising for weeks until players return without ads.
- 2025-07-06 SmartyRBX [18:58](https://youtu.be/Gml4to_jXQ0?t=1138): Fund game promotion with money: keep a job or earn from simple monetization so you can pay for ads.
- 2025-07-13 janisjanis01 [18:48](https://youtu.be/pD8fKkzF8O8?t=1128): Run small Robux-funded ads to get early players, but check you're buying ads for the correct experience.
- 2025-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/more-creators-can-now-use-rewarded-video-ads/3838678): Only users 13+ see Rewarded Video; use GetAdAvailabilityNowAsync to show the button only when an ad is available so under-13 users never see the reward option.
- 2025-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/more-creators-can-now-use-rewarded-video-ads/3838678): Expect higher EPMs with completion rates of 90%+ or Minimal content maturity; benchmark completion against the 85%+ industry expectation.
- 2025-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/more-creators-can-now-use-rewarded-video-ads/3838678): Request a test universe via the form to run Rewarded Video in a non-monetised test experience before going live.
- 2025-07-27 janisjanis01 [3:59](https://youtu.be/Sgs36l6K5EU?t=239): Test ad spend in small batches and check CTR/play rate after one day before scaling; creator spent ~4,500 Robux on first ad.
- 2025-08-20 TheDankDeveloper [10:54](https://youtu.be/OauiTUkK7og?t=654): Run a small visits campaign: 10 ad credits, US and Canada only, console unchecked, max bid 0.01, to test ROI cheaply.
- 2025-08-20 TheDankDeveloper [11:28](https://youtu.be/OauiTUkK7og?t=688): Ad spend of ~2,800 Robux on ~2,654 visits recovered only ~1,200 Robux in sales, so ads alone did not pay back immediately.
- 2025-08-24 Erthlord [7:03](https://youtu.be/dB6Us5PaWFw?t=423): Bubix suggests about 50 ad credits per day as a standard starting budget; the creator spent only 100 total, which he admits was below that.
- 2025-08-29 Roblox Creators [39:04](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=2344): Increase prominence of the rewarded prompt; Rivals got roughly 4x impressions almost overnight from a discoverability change alone.
- 2025-08-29 Roblox Creators [31:14](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1874): Prefer 6-15 second ad length; the platform accepts up to 30 seconds.
- 2025-09-07 janisjanis01 [11:31](https://youtu.be/YVKHlrlWn-k?t=691): Judge ads by CTR and playthrough rate; the creator aims for over 1% playthrough rate, and a 2.25% CTR was only average.
- 2025-09-07 janisjanis01 [12:26](https://youtu.be/YVKHlrlWn-k?t=746): Scale a campaign only after the early ad performs; the 10,000 Robux campaign gave a surge to 72 players and algorithm entry.
- 2025-09-10 Deconstructor of Fun [18:21](https://youtu.be/8pruyZlxMI4?t=1101): Ad setup reduced from about 40 steps to 3; new objectives include retention, not just visits; cost per visit reportedly under 1 cent.
- 2025-09-10 Deconstructor of Fun [26:32](https://youtu.be/8pruyZlxMI4?t=1592): Brands pay for in-game integrations (quests with rewards inside existing popular games) rather than video ads; this is a route for developers to earn brand money.
- 2025-09-10 Deconstructor of Fun [28:34](https://youtu.be/8pruyZlxMI4?t=1714): Standalone brand games usually get almost no players (over 99% have essentially no users per Row Monitor); only build one if you can expect tens of thousands of DAU.
- 2025-09-16 Deconstructor of Fun [10:19](https://youtu.be/LRyThu3bFyg?t=619): Rewarded video ads via Google require roughly 100,000 DAU before you can apply; setup is said to be simpler now.
- 2025-09-16 Deconstructor of Fun [9:37](https://youtu.be/LRyThu3bFyg?t=577): Rewarded ads suit interactive games better than banner ads; expect a learning curve integrating them cleanly into gameplay.
- 2025-09-21 janisjanis01 [3:45](https://youtu.be/uNLDi_GO2MA?t=225): Track ad CTR and QPTR per campaign; creator saw 2.12% CTR but only 0.71% QPTR and earned just 7 Robux, so low retention on ad traffic wastes spend.
- 2025-09-21 janisjanis01 [9:49](https://youtu.be/uNLDi_GO2MA?t=589): Launch ad campaigns only after the game is stable; creator spent half of launch day fixing bugs while ads were already scheduled.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [9:14](https://youtu.be/MJ-zYfL10Ew?t=554): Place rewarded video ads in high-traffic areas like lobbies or shops, and tie them to natural breaks such as level changes, not constant interruptions.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [10:35](https://youtu.be/MJ-zYfL10Ew?t=635): Give rewarded-ad viewers a meaningful benefit and prompt them clearly, since the reward is their reason to watch.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [16:29](https://youtu.be/MJ-zYfL10Ew?t=989): Spongebob TD gives a tradable cosmetic mount after 10 ad watches to engage free players without cannibalizing spenders.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [18:30](https://youtu.be/MJ-zYfL10Ew?t=1110): Ad fill is no longer a problem for Spongebob TD developers in 2025; Roblox sales team and Google back-channel gave near-full fill.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [19:52](https://youtu.be/Tese1QLD3nU?t=1192): Ads Manager integration is planned so you can target ads at audiences for individual items and for whole Shops.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [6:49](https://youtu.be/cUxgt4asjFU?t=409): Start with a small ad budget to get first players, fix UX and bugs from their behaviour, then scale spend once stats rise and retention holds.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [6:49](https://youtu.be/cUxgt4asjFU?t=409): Use ads to find players who will actually stay and enjoy the game, not just click; retention of ad-acquired users is what triggers algorithmic recommendation.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [19:37](https://youtu.be/cUxgt4asjFU?t=1177): Target ad campaigns at former players to remind them of the fun; this brought Ninja Challenge back into the home recommendation consideration set.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [24:34](https://youtu.be/cUxgt4asjFU?t=1474): Running ads does not block organic discovery per Roblox; they cite 370 advertised experiences where 99% saw total impression lift.
- 2025-09-26 Erthlord [8:26](https://youtu.be/fdDscQ6CWIs?t=506): Ad credits were spent on finished games; holding back ads for an unfinished game saves credits for launch-ready titles.
- 2025-10-11 janisjanis01 [14:26](https://youtu.be/MML_z-jpmF4?t=866): Launch small ads first to get early players and stats before scaling spend; the first campaign was used as a test to tune the game.
- 2025-10-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/analytics-view-retention-by-acquisition-source-and-select-your-benchmark-set/4010157): Ads can accelerate consideration for organic distribution and help solve the cold-start problem, but won't fix declining organic engagement.
- 2025-10-30 Roblox Creators [25:57](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=1557): Running ads should not harm organic distribution; ad users are excluded from RFY ranking signals, so ads add incremental players on top.
- 2025-10-30 Roblox Creators [27:20](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=1640): Start ad budget at a comfortable level, scale up only if results are good; check ad-user retention before spending more.
- 2025-10-30 Roblox Creators [16:24](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=984): Ads Manager offers four goals: maximize plays (launch), acquire new users, drive retention (0-30 day players), reactivate lapsed users.
- 2025-10-30 Roblox Creators [28:45](https://youtu.be/tUz_OekIIiU?t=1725): Ads are useful across the lifecycle, not only launch: use them to bring back lapsed users and grow established games.
- 2025-11-02 janisjanis01 [15:04](https://youtu.be/QnAoCsC-4ig?t=904): Monitor campaigns daily: some ads stopped before spending their ad credits, so check spend against budget.
- 2025-11-06 VuukStudios [12:14](https://youtu.be/AbjI7ygMnao?t=734): Save roughly 30k Robux for ads and run 100-200 ad credits over 1-2 weeks, spread across days rather than spent at once.
- 2025-11-06 VuukStudios [12:55](https://youtu.be/AbjI7ygMnao?t=775): Judge ad creatives by CTR; below 1% usually means the thumbnail is weak, so swap thumbnails while ads run.
- 2025-11-06 VuukStudios [14:16](https://youtu.be/AbjI7ygMnao?t=856): Track play-through rate (plays divided by impressions); aim for 1.5-2% or higher, since CTR alone does not show real plays.
- 2025-11-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rewarded-video-ads-are-now-available-to-all-ads-eligible-creators/4063278): Only show the rewarded-ad button when GetAdAvailabilityNowAsync confirms an ad is available, so players never see a dead option.
- 2025-11-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rewarded-video-ads-are-now-available-to-all-ads-eligible-creators/4063278): Reward players with a developer product's in-game item or currency. Robux cannot be given as the reward.
- 2025-11-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rewarded-video-ads-are-now-available-to-all-ads-eligible-creators/4063278): If earnings fall below forecast, the likely cause is too few ad views per user. Compute Impressions / Ads Eligible DAU (eDAU) in Analytics and raise it via placement.
- 2025-11-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rewarded-video-ads-are-now-available-to-all-ads-eligible-creators/4063278): If earnings drop to zero, check API ad-availability responses and moderation/publisher eligibility status before anything else.
- 2025-11-16 JustVeryYellow [0:40](https://youtu.be/rzB8CcRzFK8?t=40): In Ads Manager, pick the experience, choose maximize place visits, set a budget (lifetime), duration, and device/region targeting before publishing.
- 2025-11-16 JustVeryYellow [1:21](https://youtu.be/rzB8CcRzFK8?t=81): Ads targeting European regions requires accounts aged 13+ due to local laws; plan audience accordingly.
- 2025-11-16 JustVeryYellow [10:43](https://youtu.be/rzB8CcRzFK8?t=643): Measure ad performance by CTR and cost per join; creator saw ~5.69% CTR and ~0.05 ad credits per click-through join.
- 2025-11-16 JustVeryYellow [11:23](https://youtu.be/rzB8CcRzFK8?t=683): 10k Robux may be too small to make a game blow up; creator estimates 70k–100k Robux is more likely needed.
- 2025-11-23 janisjanis01 [14:16](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=856): Start ad tests with small spend and monitor CTR; creator saw 2.34% CTR on the first ad.
- 2025-12-11 bubDEV [3:29](https://youtu.be/7z8rfg_jBuk?t=209): For a new game, set ad goal to Maximize Plays, lifetime budget around 250 (range 150-400), running 5-7 days; longer runs are unnecessary.
- 2025-12-11 bubDEV [3:29](https://youtu.be/7z8rfg_jBuk?t=209): Keep ad device and location targeting at defaults (all devices); narrowing to computer/mobile/tablet cut reach significantly in his tests.
- 2025-12-12 Poke [6:54](https://youtu.be/VSVCfNHK2IE?t=414): Start ad campaigns with a small test budget and check impressions, plays and cost per play before committing the full amount.
- 2025-12-13 janisjanis01 [14:04](https://youtu.be/JYvGdKRzwEo?t=844): Run small paid ad campaigns in sequence and compare results; the third ad performed best and the weak ones were stopped.
- 2026-01-05 ItsZyonic [4:33](https://youtu.be/4-ge-E9WvM4?t=273): Start with a smaller ad budget than the ~$300/week the creator spent; scale up after making improvements, since the game wasn't retained by ads alone.
- 2026-01-18 janisjanis01 [13:03](https://youtu.be/IWzAHgHPhhU?t=783): Paid ads likely beat organic TikTok for player acquisition unless videos reach millions of views.
- 2026-01-23 Erthlord [7:34](https://youtu.be/69IBB6O4o4c?t=454): Roblox ads drove the biggest jump: a 7-day campaign at 50 ad credits/day lifted players from about 23 to 140. Check CTR and cost per play.
- 2026-02-02 Matt The Method [1:12](https://youtu.be/TEENlwjuKWI?t=72): Promote the game for free with YouTube Shorts; the speaker drove the game's earnings this way with no ad spend.
- 2026-02-03 ThePurpleGuy [1:21](https://youtu.be/VEkuM5ZG6G4?t=81): Expect paid ads to be a loss on immediate Robux; treat them as exposure and player-base building rather than payback.
- 2026-02-04 Roblox Creators [0:04](https://youtu.be/5HcH-9E7USc?t=4): Ads Manager requires users to be 13+, an ad account, and a payment method (card or Robux converted to ad credits).
- 2026-02-04 Roblox Creators [1:04](https://youtu.be/5HcH-9E7USc?t=64): For a new experience, leave audiences unselected and let Roblox find users; for an established one, target new users to grow the base.
- 2026-02-04 Roblox Creators [1:04](https://youtu.be/5HcH-9E7USc?t=64): After a major update, target lapsed users (played 30-180 days ago, not returned) to win them back.
- 2026-02-04 Roblox Creators [1:43](https://youtu.be/5HcH-9E7USc?t=103): Narrow targeting raises cost; start with a lower daily budget for about a week to test performance before expanding audience.
- 2026-02-04 Roblox Creators [2:36](https://youtu.be/5HcH-9E7USc?t=156): With stacked advanced targeting filters, lower the daily budget and run longer so the system has time to find specific users.
- 2026-02-08 janisjanis01 [11:20](https://youtu.be/2gr2TUGdNAk?t=680): Run small ad campaigns to test; the speaker's ad CTRs were 2.57% and 2.88%, and the third ad pushed the game into the algorithm.
- 2026-02-20 CallMeDogey [1:21](https://youtu.be/IQm6zE2fKUI?t=81): Advertising is one of the least important steps; prioritise benchmark improvement over ads and YouTuber outreach.
- 2026-03-06 CallMeDogey [0:00](https://youtu.be/7QfaQe4rsq8?t=0): Use Roblox's new ads system via Creator Hub > Ads; keep audience on 'all players' when advertising a new game.
- 2026-03-06 CallMeDogey [6:50](https://youtu.be/7QfaQe4rsq8?t=410): Always use a daily budget, never lifetime; steady daily player flow signals consistency and retention to the algorithm.
- 2026-03-06 CallMeDogey [7:32](https://youtu.be/7QfaQe4rsq8?t=452): Set daily budget around $5-8 and run ads at least 7 days, ideally more than 10, for consistent exposure.
- 2026-03-06 CallMeDogey [4:46](https://youtu.be/7QfaQe4rsq8?t=286): Ad cost depends on game quality and audience size; a strong game with a big audience can be advertised for roughly $50-100.
- 2026-03-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-we-are-improving-home-this-year/4502571): Ads Manager targets players likely to play the advertised game. A featured or high-performing ad moves users to playing, so RFY impressions may dip as those users move to the Continue sort; this is expected.
- 2026-03-09 GamesBeat [page](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-unveils-jumpstart-and-incubator-programs-to-help-creators-expand-possibilities-exclusive/): Roblox offers on- and off-platform user acquisition support and co-marketing programs to amplify creators' off-platform acquisition spend.
- 2026-03-27 Creators on the Blox [1:37:16](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=5836): Pursue brand and platform deals and networking with partners like Twitch; these opened event and promotion opportunities.
- 2026-04-06 Deconstructor of Fun [34:28](https://youtu.be/PNeLRjewQns?t=2068): Use paid spend to seed the algorithm: buy traffic to find your audience, let engagement data feed the algorithm, then advertising can re-kick a stale game after big updates.
- 2026-04-30 rasdev [2:01](https://youtu.be/G15TVsyC61g?t=121): Seed the algorithm with small consistent Robux ad spend, about 10k-30k Robux over 2-4 weeks, to generate initial data rather than one big spend.
- 2026-04-30 rasdev [2:01](https://youtu.be/G15TVsyC61g?t=121): Seed without Robux by getting friends to play, posting in dev communities and Discord, and making TikToks or YouTube videos. Goal is data, not just players.
- 2026-04-30 MoseyTube [4:47](https://youtu.be/sFr7htxsnR4?t=287): Run Roblox ads for about 10 days (he spent ~$10/day) so the algorithm has enough retention and spend data to push the game organically.
- 2026-05-01 LovJak [6:50](https://youtu.be/FpbZRgRHYjQ?t=410): Target ads to the days when players are most active (Friday to Sunday here) and set a daily budget to cap spend.
- 2026-05-01 LovJak [6:50](https://youtu.be/FpbZRgRHYjQ?t=410): Ad spend was lower than expected: 21 credits bought for 5,500 Robux, with only about 16 credits used over 3 days; check spend against the budget.
- 2026-05-01 Duckable [9:38](https://youtu.be/X9YYjb_mY5w?t=578): Spend on ads only after the game retains players; he spent $16/day for 10 days on ads, and said you don't need that much budget for the game to blow up.
- 2026-05-03 rasdev [7:45](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=465): Bring external traffic first (small Robux-funded ads, Discord shares, community posts, TikTok, YouTube) so the algorithm gets data to act on.
- 2026-05-10 Lnor Roblox [2:02](https://youtu.be/QOS7dQowyRk?t=122): Don't spend the whole budget at once. Creator spent ~8,000 Robux with no testing and earned ~500 back. Test with small budgets first.
- 2026-05-10 Lnor Roblox [2:02](https://youtu.be/QOS7dQowyRk?t=122): Check that the game matches what the ad promises. Higher playtime but worse D1 retention from ads suggests audience or messaging mismatch.
- 2026-05-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/alternate-publishing-requirements-for-roblox-kids-and-select/4630944): Promote your game to users over 16; engagement from age-checked 16+ users speeds up Kids/Select evaluation.
- 2026-05-13 ThePurpleGuy [0:00](https://youtu.be/JXg2ACdk_wM?t=0): Make sure the game is fully ready before buying ads; the spend is committed once credits are used.
- 2026-05-16 Tizzy [12:12](https://youtu.be/inWzxY26NXo?t=732): Don't run more ads on a dying game without stat improvements; sponsored ad traffic doesn't push you up the home algorithm on its own.
- 2026-05-16 Tizzy [17:23](https://youtu.be/inWzxY26NXo?t=1043): Don't keep restarting ads once on algo; Roblox uses a rolling 7-day data window, so big updates take a week to show fully.
- 2026-05-16 Tizzy [4:45](https://youtu.be/inWzxY26NXo?t=285): Expect low stats from cold ad traffic; ads optimise for the cheapest player, not the most qualified, so don't panic over bot-like lobbies.
- 2026-05-16 Alex Builds Games [8:15](https://youtu.be/wb8XBVFI2i4?t=495): Use Roblox ads to gather fast data early rather than relying on SEO, which the speaker calls a slow, weaker main traffic driver.
- 2026-05-16 Alex Builds Games [8:55](https://youtu.be/wb8XBVFI2i4?t=535): Run ads for about 7 days so Roblox's learning phase (about 2 days, then optimizing over 7) finds your most engaged audience, then stop them.
- 2026-05-16 Alex Builds Games [10:18](https://youtu.be/wb8XBVFI2i4?t=618): After ads, stop spending and let home recommendations fill the game; the algorithm targets players similar to those who engaged with your ad traffic.
- 2026-05-23 Tizzy [12:39](https://youtu.be/owMnhg3fn_M?t=759): Start ads at $16/day on daily budget, run at least 3 days (ideally a week), All Players Recommended, all ages.
- 2026-05-23 Tizzy [15:03](https://youtu.be/owMnhg3fn_M?t=903): Keep running ads until home recommendation impressions surpass sponsored ad impressions in Acquisition analytics, then stop.
- 2026-05-30 Arow [0:40](https://youtu.be/BxK28jVj1MY?t=40): Set ad campaign goal to Plays, audience to All Players; use Recent Players if the aim is bringing lapsed players back.
- 2026-05-30 Arow [1:00](https://youtu.be/BxK28jVj1MY?t=60): Run ads continuously at about 45 ad credits per day daily budget for roughly one week.
- 2026-05-30 Yann [2:02](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=122): Use Roblox Ads Manager as the core promotion tool; use daily budget, maximize plays, all regions and all devices.
- 2026-05-30 Yann [2:43](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=163): Set ad budget by three factors: trend urgency, whether the game needs multiplayer, and genre supply versus demand.
- 2026-05-30 Yann [3:25](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=205): Simulators need less ad spend than multiplayer games like tag or knockout, and niche genres like racing need more.
- 2026-05-31 Duckable [22:12](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=1332): Track ad return: 37,000 Robux on ads returned only about 10,000 Robux in the creator's first 24-hour game.
- 2026-06-01 Deconstructor of Fun [30:49](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=1849): Test a new country with a small paid UA campaign first. Scale to influencers only if retention, CPI or ROI look good, then move to always-on.
- 2026-06-01 Deconstructor of Fun [31:30](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=1890): Vet local agencies through case studies. Agencies know local creators and often get better rates, and they reduce coordination overhead for small teams.
- 2026-06-01 Deconstructor of Fun [34:16](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2056): Avoid TikTok for influencer attribution: no link-in-bio or sticker link, so you must pay Spark Ads on top of creator fees, which doubles spend.
- 2026-06-01 Deconstructor of Fun [43:13](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2593): Set expectations up front: first campaigns are for learning, and influencer marketing needs budget and time before it becomes profitable.
- 2026-06-01 Deconstructor of Fun [4:47](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=287): Keep influencer marketing as a permanent pillar rather than a one-off test. Cutting spend increased install decline in one 10-year-old game.
- 2026-06-01 Deconstructor of Fun [34:16](https://youtu.be/2BsMLCk8FEU?t=2056): Avoid TikTok for link-based acquisition if it lacks a usable link placement; spark ads mean paying both the creator and media spend.
- 2026-06-01 Tizzy [5:06](https://youtu.be/Rkgbcv6omPg?t=306): Ads served to 16+ automatically; you don't need to set targeting to 16+ to hit the threshold.
- 2026-06-01 Tizzy [8:48](https://youtu.be/Rkgbcv6omPg?t=528): Ads brought most traffic early, but many ad plays were bots that never move; expect low-quality ad traffic.
- 2026-06-01 Tizzy [11:25](https://youtu.be/Rkgbcv6omPg?t=685): Budget roughly $16/day for ~11 days (~$180 total) to clear the threshold; the speaker says this is only slightly more than prior algo-hitting spend.
- 2026-06-01 Tizzy [12:05](https://youtu.be/Rkgbcv6omPg?t=725): If ad budget is too tight, earn ad money via commissions rather than skipping ads.
- 2026-06-01 MoseyTube [0:19](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=19): Run ads at launch: Roblox won't recommend a new game until its retention and return-play data look good, and ads supply that data.
- 2026-06-01 MoseyTube [1:00](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=60): Start with $10/day for 10 days ($100 total); raise to $15–$20/day for competitive genres or games needing more players to be playable.
- 2026-06-01 MoseyTube [1:53](https://youtu.be/d9UxSCiz3us?t=113): Keep campaign settings broad: maximize plays, all regions, all devices, and let the algorithm find the audience rather than hunting for targeting tricks.
- 2026-06-06 janisjanis01 [10:06](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=606): Run paid ad campaigns in Ad Manager; spend in tranches (e.g. 28,000 Robux first campaign) and measure each before the next.
- 2026-06-10 MoseyTube [3:16](https://youtu.be/esZQX5COI_E?t=196): When re-advertising an old game, the algorithm already has data from earlier runs, so a short $10/day test can show it an updated version without starting from zero.
- 2026-06-15 rasdev [3:23](https://youtu.be/7VhmLFkhwPU?t=203): Don't run ads to fix an unoptimized game; the speaker says it feeds Roblox bad data.
- 2026-06-15 rasdev [3:23](https://youtu.be/7VhmLFkhwPU?t=203): Use paid ads only to get enough initial players so Roblox has numbers on the game, then rely on organic stats.
- 2026-06-18 DitchyDevelopments [0:41](https://youtu.be/cfLbW2j2VLs?t=41): Don't buy ads until the game has solid gameplay and retention; the speaker's ad test peaked at ~20 players then fell to near zero within an hour.
- 2026-06-29 MoseyTube [3:34](https://youtu.be/Kh_XKYw8ocU?t=214): Run Roblox ads at about $10/day for 10 days ($100 total) with maximize plays, all regions, all devices to feed the algorithm data.
- 2026-07-08 rasdev [2:43](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=163): Don't spend on ads to fix a game problem; ads mask weak retention and players will still leave.
- 2026-07-09 LovJak [4:06](https://youtu.be/feVPsViqceo?t=246): Set a custom daily ad budget and run for a fixed number of days so spend is capped; the creator used 10 credits/day for 3 days.
- 2026-07-09 LovJak [4:06](https://youtu.be/feVPsViqceo?t=246): Turn off auto-reload of ad credits to avoid unplanned spending.
- 2026-07-13 Tizzy [0:00](https://youtu.be/nggNvUILQ1I?t=0): Start running ads about two weeks after launch. Creator saw growth after ads began, though no spend figures were given.
- 2026-07-17 VuukStudios [12:26](https://youtu.be/Oo3sCq-TQXs?t=746): Turn off paid ads once home recommendations take over; the speaker shut ads off to let organic recommendations carry the game.
- 2026-07-23 Yann [2:11](https://youtu.be/Bw2ty2T46z4?t=131): Pay for ads only while engaged players are arriving; the creator ran ads 19 days at $20/day and said they should have stopped sooner.
- 2026-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/leveling-up-ads-manager-with-new-features/4755120): Use Gen AI Creatives in Ads Manager to test multiple ad creative directions quickly without a full production cycle.
- 2026-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/leveling-up-ads-manager-with-new-features/4755120): Upload pre-moderated game assets or images as references so generated creatives match your characters, colors and visual style.
- 2026-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/leveling-up-ads-manager-with-new-features/4755120): Store proven creatives in the Asset Library so they can be reused across campaigns instead of tied to a single campaign.
- 2026-07-26 Lnor Roblox [4:55](https://youtu.be/U6gv03QGcDs?t=295): Check that ad clicks can become plays: unverified users were clicking ads they could not enter, wasting spend.
- 2026-07-26 Lnor Roblox [6:17](https://youtu.be/U6gv03QGcDs?t=377): Target verified players aged 18-24 on PC and mobile; the same thumbnails rose from 1.5% to over 8% CTR in a day.
- 2026-07-29 VuukStudios [6:31](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=391): Buy Robux ads only once the core loop is done; a good game can convert cheap ad spend (e.g. 5k Robux for 10k players), while a bad one gets almost no players.
- 2026-07-31 LovJak [2:41](https://youtu.be/Y602HH_KdOc?t=161): Set campaign to all-players goal, daily budget of 12 ad credits, run continuously, and disable auto-reload to cap spend.
- 2026-08-02 janisjanis01 [5:24](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=324): Make the game public, then run first ad campaign: goal Players, all-player audience, 8 ad credits/day for 2 days, auto-reload off, 2-3 thumbnails.
- 2026-08-02 janisjanis01 [6:05](https://youtu.be/fjlOaEALWmg?t=365): After the first campaign, launch a second at 16 ad credits/day for 2 days if the first shows traction.
- 2026-08-06 Raul [4:04](https://youtu.be/7vYv8DA7pqU?t=244): Roblox does not let you swap thumbnails on an active ad; you must cancel and restart, losing its momentum. Get creatives right first.
- 2026-08-06 Raul [2:12](https://youtu.be/7vYv8DA7pqU?t=132): Run ad campaigns for 7 days rather than 5; he heard longer runs learn better but cut budget short.
- 2026-08-06 Raul [0:15](https://youtu.be/7vYv8DA7pqU?t=15): Posting free YouTube Shorts drove over 1,000 views but barely moved visits (max ~20 players); paid reach was needed.
- 2026-08-06 Raul [2:44](https://youtu.be/7vYv8DA7pqU?t=164): Check revenue under the group, not personal account, when the game is group-owned.
- 2026-08-07 MoseyTube [2:06](https://youtu.be/5zz5ao0zV_Y?t=126): Stop paying for ads once the game gets home-page recommendations; the algorithm then carries traffic organically, so ongoing ad spend is unnecessary.
- 2026-08-07 LovJak [7:31](https://youtu.be/KZ_mxVrvHCI?t=451): Track reach thresholds: he needed about 500 highly engaged players to unlock all-ages ad targeting, and had 300. Expect ads to reach a narrow audience until then.
- 2026-08-07 RoyalDev [14:17](https://youtu.be/RXmiJkaZWV4?t=857): Run ads consistently rather than repeatedly switching them on and off; the creator's stop-start ad pattern hurt results.
- 2026-08-15 RoyalDev [1:21](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=81): Budget, daily vs lifetime, and start time barely matter; pick the experience, choose engagement or audience targeting, and start.
- 2026-08-15 RoyalDev [2:43](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=163): Let ads run continuously. Roblox spends about 24 hours learning after publish; pulling and reposting resets that learning.
- 2026-08-15 RoyalDev [2:43](https://youtu.be/mLMXeMWmTwU?t=163): Don't judge ads after 2-3 days with on/off cycling; this created the worst results for the speaker's last two games.
- 2026-08-18 MonarchDev [5:02](https://youtu.be/MANBsA1MTzI?t=302): Test Roblox ads with small budgets and track results; the speaker lost a couple hundred dollars across two games with no return.
- 2026-08-23 MonarchDev [2:45](https://youtu.be/jX3E9OHBeTg?t=165): Ad campaign reached 120,000 impressions but only ~3,000 clicks and ~1,000 plays. Expect low click-to-play conversion and budget accordingly.
- 2026-08-23 MonarchDev [4:47](https://youtu.be/jX3E9OHBeTg?t=287): Track CTR across ad creatives; second campaign had higher click-through rate than the first.
- 2026-08-25 RoyalDev [1:22](https://youtu.be/6CefFrCFqcI?t=82): Don't pour money into ads before retention and session length are good; ads only scale what already works and burn cash on weak games.
- 2026-08-25 MoseyTube [2:40](https://youtu.be/xIBc5He_sy4?t=160): Run ads with a set budget until the game either flops or home recommendations appear; he spent about $148 before home recs on Steal a Superstar.
- 2026-08-26 VuukStudios [1:45](https://youtu.be/m7015JSIrpw?t=105): Budget ads for 1-2 weeks, roughly 30K-60K Robux (~$100-200) total, not per day; the algorithm won't pick up a game that just sits.
- 2026-08-30 janisjanis01 [17:44](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=1064): Ads spent heavily with little result: 13,000 Robux for ~35 highly engaged players. Test small budgets before scaling.
- 2026-09-01 VuukStudios [3:23](https://youtu.be/2Q4NqQikzsY?t=203): Run ads again on each major update of a long-running game to trigger a new algorithm push; the push itself is largely luck-based.
- 2026-09-06 kra_DeZ [10:28](https://youtu.be/cJ-YzHHhQs8?t=628): Before big spend, generate 20+ AI thumbnails and A/B test pairs with a short ad run (about 3 days, lifetime budget) to pick the highest-CTR one.
- 2026-09-06 kra_DeZ [11:08](https://youtu.be/cJ-YzHHhQs8?t=668): After the winning thumbnail, run a larger ad burst (~90-100 ad credits over 4-5 days); expect the player spike to drop back to near zero after the ads end.
- 2026-09-08 ItsZyonic [5:04](https://youtu.be/RIWPfMJyVhQ?t=304): Ads alone may not catch the algorithm; the speaker turned ads off and organic metrics then climbed and CCU followed.
- 2026-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-provided-discounts-on-in-game-items/4861443): Watch for the blue notification indicator on the Shop button; it signals new offers are available to players.
- 2026-09-11 MonarchDev [2:43](https://youtu.be/LD7LGP10dRc?t=163): Paid ads alone brought visits but not retained players; ~$130 spend gave 8,800 plays from 641,000 impressions with no lasting players.
- 2026-09-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-roblox-wallet-and-card-get-paid-faster/4865505): Wallet funds can be spent directly on buying ads, in addition to Robux.
- 2026-09-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880): Pre-roll video ads (coming soon) need no code; Roblox optimizes delivery and shares revenue. Roblox Plus subscribers won't see them.
- 2026-09-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880): Pre-roll ads (coming soon) need no code; Roblox optimizes delivery and shares revenue. Roblox Plus subscribers won't see them.
- 2026-09-12 LovJak [2:46](https://youtu.be/VHdyHvHOId8?t=166): Start ad campaigns with an engagement objective targeting all players, and reuse the thumbnail that previously performed best (creator says banana thumbnail won before).
- 2026-09-13 VuukStudios [6:41](https://youtu.be/eS0Vc7h0VW0?t=401): Run Roblox ads for 1–2 weeks to get initial players and let the algorithm pick up; prefer Roblox ads over TikTok/Shorts for early traffic.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [1:42:00](https://youtu.be/3a2RbLOAl4A?t=6120): Integrate rewarded video; 27% of games that did so earned more than 10% of revenue from it. 71 of top 100 games run sponsored ads.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [1:21:43](https://youtu.be/3a2RbLOAl4A?t=4903): Pre-roll ads (short, skippable, shown during load) are being tested; Roblox handles delivery and revenue share with no integration.
- 2026-09-23 VuukStudios [13:08](https://youtu.be/d5YHwCqwgYM?t=788): Run ads for 1-2 weeks with a 30k-60k Robux (about $100-$200) budget; if the game gets no traction, cut it and start another idea.
- 2026-09-30 Roblox Investor Relations [page](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm): Rewarded Video and Sponsored Tiles show product-market fit; 20%+ of rewarded-video games earned over 10% of in-game earnings from ads.
- 2026-09-30 Roblox Investor Relations [page](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001315098/000162828026068235/ex991-q32026earningsshar.htm): Paid user acquisition targeting O18 users retained, engaged and monetized better than organic signups; keep ROI bar high while scaling.
- 2026-09-30 RoyalDev [2:02](https://youtu.be/rxeLNsNUVTQ?t=122): Ads display your thumbnails, so make them clear and high click-through; they are often the visual players judge the game by.
- 2026-10-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ads-manager-rdc-recap-earnings-roas-reporting-incrementality/4909790): Earnings objective targets players likelier to spend; use it only for well-monetized games, since Plays objective is better for cheap plays.
- 2026-10-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ads-manager-rdc-recap-earnings-roas-reporting-incrementality/4909790): Reinvesting up to 4% of earnings into ads: nearly two of three such games reached ROAS above 100%, per Roblox.
- 2026-10-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ads-manager-rdc-recap-earnings-roas-reporting-incrementality/4909790): Use Estimated ROAS to judge Earnings campaigns early instead of pausing or waiting up to 30 days to adjust spend.
- 2026-10-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ads-manager-rdc-recap-earnings-roas-reporting-incrementality/4909790): Keep campaign funding steady with auto-reload; each pause restarts the 24-48 hour learning period.
- 2026-10-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ads-manager-rdc-recap-earnings-roas-reporting-incrementality/4909790): Start ads with a small budget and scale; Ink Game reported ads raised CCU and metrics once they began scaling.
- 2026-10-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ads-manager-rdc-recap-earnings-roas-reporting-incrementality/4909790): Group campaigns: create a new campaign with Group Ad Credit as funding source; campaigns cannot be transferred between accounts.
- 2026-10-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-september-28%E2%80%93october-2-2026/4910267): Consider the Ads Manager Earnings objective, now available to nearly 30,000 games, and check the improved ROAS reporting to evaluate ad spend.
- 2026-10-03 MonarchDev [10:36](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=636): Set explicit win criteria before ads go live: beat the Robux spend, get players returning, and pay back ad spend.
- 2026-10-03 MonarchDev [10:36](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=636): Ad credits can be bought with Robux, about 1,000 Robux for roughly $3.80 in ad credits. Small budgets buy few players.
- 2026-10-04 Seibo [0:43](https://youtu.be/FmAD02FS_3k?t=43): Lower sponsored ad credits once organic home-recommendation players exceed ad-driven players; he cut spend at that crossover point.
- 2026-10-09 Rapstrike GFX [0:00](https://youtu.be/7yNr2prDUsE?t=0): Spend roughly $130 over 10 days on Roblox ads to test a game; he got ~100 average CCU from it.
- 2026-10-09 Alex Builds Games [13:01](https://youtu.be/ntjVykMw0As?t=781): Treat each game like a media-buying test: vary the audience/avatar per product, as paid social advertisers do, to find winners.
- 2026-10-11 rasdev [9:36](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=576): Ads can raise player counts but not ranking; judge ad traffic by day-7 retention and play sessions, not impressions or clicks.
- 2026-10-11 rasdev [10:16](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=616): A campaign with high impressions but few plays signals a weak game; fix retention before spending more on ads.
- 2026-10-11 rasdev [10:57](https://youtu.be/e4o-ECkzDoI?t=657): Qualified-player threshold for Roblox Kids and Select campaigns is now 250 engaged players, down from 500 (changed August of this year per speaker).
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [15:10](https://youtu.be/bkRZeTZAvLw?t=910): Run launch ads to gather enough users for funnel and economy data; small samples are not useful.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [9:38](https://youtu.be/hoMbv_R8AMU?t=578): Add rewarded video to monetise non-paying players; it is opt-in and Roblox reports it does not cannibalise in-game spend.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [11:02](https://youtu.be/hoMbv_R8AMU?t=662): Use the Studio plugin to add rewarded video from templates in minutes; use multi-reward rotation to avoid reward fatigue.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [13:39](https://youtu.be/hoMbv_R8AMU?t=819): Use ads to break past the recommendation ceiling; paid impressions can extend reach beyond what RFY alone delivers.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [15:42](https://youtu.be/hoMbv_R8AMU?t=942): Try the earnings objective with ads manager; it optimises for ROAS, and estimated ROAS now gives an earlier signal than the 30-day window.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [27:14](https://youtu.be/hoMbv_R8AMU?t=1634): Scale ad budget gradually while ROAS stays above 100%; InGame doubled then tripled spend from $250/day and kept returns.
- 2026-10-17 rasdev [2:17](https://youtu.be/DtdGtvALhCA?t=137): Only pay for ads once the game already holds players; ads don't rescue a weak game. Roblox tests reported +14% play sessions and +10% week-later retention (median, 21 campaigns).
- 2026-10-20 kra_DeZ [4:52](https://youtu.be/lrubtajkaeI?t=292): Run a cheap test campaign first, then a longer main campaign using only the winning thumbnail, lasting about 4–5 days.
- 2026-10-20 kra_DeZ [6:16](https://youtu.be/lrubtajkaeI?t=376): Set ad campaigns to optimise for Plays, audience All Players, and use a lifetime budget in ad credits.
- 2026-10-20 kra_DeZ [7:39](https://youtu.be/lrubtajkaeI?t=459): Schedule the next campaign to start right when the previous one ends so no ad time is wasted.
- 2026-10-20 kra_DeZ [8:21](https://youtu.be/lrubtajkaeI?t=501): Only advertise games whose organic stats (retention, playtime) are already good; he says many of his earlier games failed despite ads.
- 2026-10-22 Deconstructor of Fun [19:02](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=1142): Only scale user acquisition when ROAS-positive; creator activations driving download uplift do not prove ad profitability.
- 2026-10-23 tef [14:28](https://youtu.be/OfObWnrucRY?t=868): Expect a slow start on paid ads: the speaker says the Roblox ad algorithm needs time to learn the target audience before pushing to the right players.
- 2026-10-23 rasdev [5:26](https://youtu.be/RC19PKdRt1Y?t=326): The new engagement ad goal is in beta and can overspend; pause it manually after reaching the engaged-player target. Ads reportedly don't change organic ranking.
- 2026-10-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-october-19%E2%80%9323-2026/4953263): Sponsored Ad and Recommended For You tiles can now both show on Home, giving players two ways to find your game; see the Discovery FAQ.
- 2026-10-26 Deconstructor of Fun [36:32](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2192): Target lapsed players with ads; a 40k-player campaign returned 50% within a week at under $2 cost per activation (claimed).
- 2026-10-26 Deconstructor of Fun [41:14](https://youtu.be/NW4EShVGPYs?t=2474): Expect multi-touch conversion: she cites roughly eight impressions to drive an action, so judge ads on repeated exposure, not last touch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/advertise-on-roblox): Pick the ad product by content type: Immersive ads for games/videos/images, Search ads for game tiles by keyword, Sponsored games for 9+/13+/all-ages games, Sponsored items for UGC.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/advertise-on-roblox): Roblox-served ads let you choose audience, schedule duration, and set a daily budget.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/advertise-on-roblox): If your in-game content promotes an off-Roblox product or service, treat it as independent advertising and follow the Independent Advertising Policy.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/advertise-on-roblox): Independent ads must be hidden from users ineligible to receive ads and clearly disclosed as advertising.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/advertise-on-roblox): For brand or sponsor deals, consult regulatory guidance and seek legal counsel when unsure whether content counts as advertising.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads): Only public games with a 13+ owner, ID verification, and 2FA are eligible; game needs 2,000 unique monthly visitors (bots excluded).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads): Complete the Maturity and Compliance Questionnaire; resubmitting temporarily removes ads eligibility, so only resubmit when a game update changes prior answers.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads): Scale billboard ad blocks between 8x4.5 and 32x18 studs; out-of-range ad units are invalid and cannot serve ads.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads): Do not add a second AdGui or SurfaceGui on the same ad face; it invalidates the ad unit.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads): Portal ad BasePortal package: only scale, position, and rotation may change; other edits invalidate it and it stops serving.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads): Pair click-to-play video ads with a rewarded video mechanism to drive users to watch at least 15 seconds.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads): Ineligible users see a Roblox logo fallback by default; to comply with Advertising Standards, hide, replace, or block ads for them using PolicyService:GetPolicyInfoForPlayerAsync AreAdsAllowed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/immersive-ads): Destroying ad units for some users can change gameplay if other users still see them; account for this in design.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion): Set a per-day budget and schedule and choose your audience when running sponsored game or item campaigns; search ads can target keywords.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/deeplinks): Use deep links with launchData for traffic attribution and promotional codes so players land in a specific place with context.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/auth/api-keys): placeholder
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-notifications): Notifications are throttled by engagement: more clicks means more reach, so write high-CTR messages.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/share-links): Create a separate share link for each off-platform channel so acquisition metrics can be compared per channel.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/share-links): For group-owned games, create share links from the group account and ensure you have the required group permission; you must own the game to create links.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/monetization): For brand integrations, choose partners matching your game's vibe, aesthetics and goals; limited activations usually run 2-4 weeks.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/monetization): Weave brand content into the core loop so it feels like a natural extension of gameplay, enhancing progression.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/acquisition): Only run acquisition campaigns or event announcements once retention and engagement are good, since paid traffic is wasted on weak retention.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing): Card payments are charged when the $100 threshold is reached, otherwise at month end; daily ad spend cap on cards is $300.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing): Ad credit campaigns have no daily spend limit; unused credit is refunded to ad credit balance 24-48 hours after campaign end, never to Robux.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/reporting-and-billing): Fraud refunds are assessed 14 days after campaign end and applied 16 days after if invalid traffic is detected; budget for this delay.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/private-servers): Private servers are useful for friends, classes, meetings, streaming without others, and gathering resources; design for these use cases.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/obby): Offer timed buffs or extra daily-login bonuses via Rewarded Video on first spawn to monetize new players early.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/obby): Use Rewarded Video mid-session to give free currency or trials of high-value items during play.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/obby): After failure, offer a second chance that preserves progress (e.g., respawn at previous checkpoint or claim a one-life item).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/obby): Show the rewarded video prompt when the game starts or when a round ends so it is visible.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/obby): State the reward clearly in the prompt; do not label it just 'ad'so players know what they get.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/obby): Stack rewards or offer multiple engagement points to raise daily views per user.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/tools): Use Sponsored Ads audience selection (demographics, interests) and flexible daily budgets to target players and manage ad spend.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production): Use Ads Manager image, video, or portal ads to promote a game inside existing experiences.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/advertise): Use Ads Manager 'Plays' campaigns targeting All Players and Recent Players to drive plays; Hypershot says sponsored ads reached up to 10% of its plays.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/advertise): Ramp ad traffic up or down to test features, support live updates, or scale; run always-on campaigns to optimise the user funnel.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations): Register campaigns early and submit at least 5 days before the campaign start date.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations): Submit all ad assets to Roblox for review before exposing them to users; moderation typically completes within 48 hours.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations): Clearly and prominently disclose ads in simple language; use standardized Ad labels within 500 studs and frequent, large labels if players 12 or under are targeted.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations): Do not use misleading content to drive engagement, such as offering free Robux. Violations risk account suspension or loss of ad eligibility.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations): Show rewarded video ads only to users 13 and older.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations): Add new assets to a separate campaign with fixed dates instead of an active campaign; during review the whole campaign serves only 18+ users.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations): Check eligibility with AdService:GetCampaignEligibilityAsync; a rejected asset makes it return false for all users.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ad-integrations): Self-promotional assets, such as merchandise or off-platform products with calls to action, also count as ads and need registration.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/why-build-on-roblox): Use the paid UA system to acquire users and review traffic-source insights to optimise your user acquisition strategy.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox): Design with streamers in mind: make it easy for them to involve friends, and consider features like hiding UI for recording (Brookhaven's Creator Cam).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items): Sponsored items promote Marketplace items only; to advertise an experience use Ads Manager or the Advertising API.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items): Display share is proportional to Daily Budget relative to other bidders; doubling your bid roughly doubles impressions versus a rival's.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items): Set a target audience (gender, age, platform) and a descriptive ad name when creating a sponsored item; the name is only visible to you.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/marketplace/sponsor-items): Roblox holds the full ad-cycle amount on Run. Cancelling refunds each full day left, so cancel early to recover unused days.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/search-ads): Bid only on keywords most relevant to your game; the auction weights relevance, so irrelevant keywords (e.g. horror game bidding on 'obby') get little sustained traffic.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/search-ads): Group related keywords within each Ad Set (max 10 per Ad Set) for clearer performance reporting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/search-ads): Create single-keyword Ad Sets to compare how an individual keyword performs, since keyword filtering isn't available in reports.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/search-ads): Search ads must follow Community Standards; Roblox suppresses games identified as clickbait or spam.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/search-ads): Search ads use the Visits campaign objective; you cannot modify brand suitability or audience targeting for them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/incremental-sim): Track opt-in rate and daily frequency as the two key metrics driving rewarded video earnings.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/incremental-sim): Use rewarded video to bridge friction (waiting time between upgrades) for non-paying players while keeping whales on premium options.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/incremental-sim): Use multiple rewarded video placements; multi-placement strategies work best.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/incremental-sim): Keep the rewarded video UI clean with a clear value proposition.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/incremental-sim): Balance rewards for player value against economic impact on your game economy.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/sports): Place rewarded video prompts on high-traffic surfaces: HUD, pre-match lobby, and loss screen. Buried placements kill engagement rates.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/sports): Time ad triggers to emotional moments: before a match (anticipation), after a loss (frustration), after a win (reward). Contextual timing beats a static button.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/sports): Offer post-match ad option to double coins or XP just earned. Players are invested in their result, win or lose.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/sports): Add a free-currency-via-ad option in the store/HUD next to paid coin bundles so ads feel like earning, not interruption.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/sports): On the loss screen, offer an ad to instantly re-enter a match instead of waiting. Targets loss aversion.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/sports): Make ad-bought boosts last one match or one session only. Permanent rewards create pay-to-win perception and hurt competitive balance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/survival): Track opt-in rate and daily frequency as the two key rewarded video metrics; target 13%+ opt-in and 2.5+ views per user per day.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/survival): Offer a revive or respawn near the death spot on the death screen, when loss aversion peaks.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/survival): Place a pre-round power-up offer (shield, speed boost, tool) in the lobby; it lasts one round and drives repeat views each session.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/survival): Offer to preserve an active win or survival streak via ad when a player dies; this targets your most engaged users.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/survival): Set a daily engagement cap on rewarded video to prevent farming and keep rewards feeling special.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/rpg): Frame rewarded video as paying with time: reward time saved (e.g., bypass crafting cooldown), not power.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/rpg): Trigger rewarded ads at loss-aversion moments, e.g., watch a 15s video to revive at checkpoint instead of starting village.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/rpg): Use scarcity placements: offer hard-to-farm items like a Luck Potion boosting rare drops for 10 minutes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-playbooks/rpg): Use grind placements such as timed 2x EXP boosts to speed progression and lift session length and retention.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads): Call GetAdAvailabilityNowAsync as close as possible to showing the ad (e.g., when the shop opens), not earlier, to improve fill rate and CPM.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads): After an ad finishes, only call GetAdAvailabilityNowAsync if you will offer another ad opportunity, to avoid filled requests that never produce impressions.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads): Disclose clearly before each ad what the reward is and what the user must do, e.g., 'Watch the ad to earn 10 diamonds!' on the button.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads): Grant the reward immediately after the ad finishes; never force ads as a progress gate or take over screen or avatar motion.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads): Use placements (Monetization > Ads > Placements) and pass the Placement ID to track each ad position separately.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads): Register ad buttons with RegisterAdOpportunityAsync and compare filled requests, opportunities, and impressions to decide whether to make ads easier to find or rewards more compelling.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-ads): Meet eligibility: 13+, ID-verified, 2FA enabled, public unrestricted game with at least 2,000 average unique visitors per month, and approved maturity questionnaire.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/comply-with-advertising-standards): Ensure all ad content is policy-compliant and clearly disclosed as advertising in users' language; do not mimic Roblox's official ad disclosures (different font/color).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/comply-with-advertising-standards): Only serve ad content you uploaded yourself inside your game; do not call third-party ad servers, even for analytics.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/comply-with-advertising-standards): Call PolicyService:GetPolicyInfoForPlayerAsync per player and check AreAdsAllowed; if false, hide, replace, or block ads for that user.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/comply-with-advertising-standards): If the whole game is an ad, block ineligible users (AreAdsAllowed false) from entering, e.g. kick with a message.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/comply-with-advertising-standards): Use Roblox localization tools to make ad disclosures readable across languages.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/roblox-user-base): Strong organic traffic and low friction keep user acquisition (UA) costs low; prioritise organic growth before paid UA.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies): Spend earned Robux on ad campaigns rather than purchased Robux, since the exchange rate is higher for earned Robux.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising): Before creating a campaign, call campaign-options for the universe to confirm eligibility; NO_PERMISSION or BLOCKED means it can't advertise.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising): Send a UUID in x-idempotency-key on campaign creation; replaying the same key with an identical body within 24 hours returns the original campaign, avoiding duplicates.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising): New campaigns start as IN_REVIEW until ad-policy review completes; poll campaigns:batchGetStatus (up to 100 IDs per call) until SERVING, NOT_SERVING or REJECTED.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising): Use durationInDays, not endTime, to set campaign length; endTime returns a 400 in v1.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising): Budget decreases on running campaigns apply at next midnight in the billing account time zone; increases apply immediately, so raise budgets carefully.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising): Cancelling a campaign (status CANCELLED) is permanent; use PAUSED to temporarily stop spend and ACTIVE to resume.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising): Use targeting arrays (ageGroups, countries, devices) to narrow audiences; an empty array means all for that dimension.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/advertising): Creatives must be Open Cloud image assets uploaded via the Assets API; reference their IDs in creativeAssetIds.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences): For a limited time test, consider spending roughly $100-250 USD on Roblox ad space to drive traffic to the test window.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager): Expect Engagement campaigns to have higher CPP; use a dedicated budget rather than shifting spend from Plays campaigns.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager): Allow 3–5 days for campaign results; if no progress by then, improve onboarding and first-time user experience before continuing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager): Ad campaigns for games that are not unique can result in no ad spend and no conversions; metadata and place files resembling existing games rank lower.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager): Use Plays objective for broad distribution, testing new ideas, or initial traffic that can help get Recommended For You consideration.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager): Use Earnings objective only if your game has a well-developed in-game economy; it targets high-value spenders.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager): Use launch data to tailor onboarding per ad audience, spawn players at the ad's map, or grant ad-only rewards; exclusive rewards help conversion.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager): Test the launch URL before publishing a campaign with launch data; GetJoinData must be wired into onPlayerAdded.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager): Running ads does not affect Recommended For You ranking, so ads and organic recommendations are independent.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager): Use Recent Players or Lapsed Players audiences only if the game meets 10,000 recent or 20,000 lapsed player minimums.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager): Cancel a campaign up to 6 hours before scheduled start to get ad credits back and avoid card charges.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager): Set auto-reload carefully: it reloads one day's worth of ad credits and continues daily to keep the campaign running.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/ads-manager): Payment method cannot be changed after a campaign is published; choose it before publishing.
- 2026-10-29 Deconstructor of Fun [6:46](https://youtu.be/NrPjXO_7oKk?t=406): Discord campaigns for existing games are roughly half reactivation; use it to win back lapsed players.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/rewarded-video-optimization): Show the RV prompt within the first couple of actions after the triggering moment, not buried in a menu.
